### [88] [Oscillating Neutrinos vs. Oscillating Scalars: Constraining Scalar Dark Matter-Induced Neutrino Mass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06460)
*Kierthika Chathirathas,Sabya Sachi Chatterjee,Thomas Schwetz*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了中微子质量由超轻（伪）标量场耦合产生的假设，该标量场同时构成暗物质（dark matter）。结果显示，对于暗物质质量在约3×10⁻⁸eV至10⁻²¹eV范围内，中微子总质量中仅有9%至54%可源自标量场耦合，未来JUNO实验数据可能将这一限制改善至1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究试图将中微子质量起源与暗物质联系起来，通过假设两者由同一超轻标量场耦合产生，以解释中微子质量的动态变化及其对振荡实验数据的影响。

Method: 作者假设中微子质量通过耦合到构成暗物质的超轻（伪）标量场产生，考虑银河系中暗物质处于维里化（virialised）状态，并详细讨论相关时间尺度。利用T2K、RENO和JUNO三个实验的数据对模型进行约束分析；模型主要基于实数标量场，但多数结论同样适用于复数标量场。

Result: 结果表明，对于暗物质质量范围在约3×10⁻⁸eV至最小允许质量10⁻²¹eV之间，中微子总质量中仅有9%至54%的份额可源自背景标量场的耦合，该比例取决于标量质量的具体取值。未来JUNO数据在特定标量质量区间可将约束改进至1%。

Conclusion: 该模型可对中微子质量源于暗物质标量场耦合的比例给出明确上限约束，且目前实验数据并不支持全部中微子质量均来自该机制；未来JUNO实验有望显著提高约束精度。

Abstract: We consider the hypothesis that neutrino masses are generated by a coupling to an ultra-light (pseudo-)scalar field, which provides the dark matter in the universe. This leads to time-varying neutrino masses with a frequency set by the dark matter mass, with implications for neutrino oscillation data. We use that dark matter is in a virialised state in the galaxy and provide a detailed discussion of the relevant time scales. Using data from the T2K, RENO and JUNO experiments, we show that for dark matter masses smaller than about $3\times 10^{-8}$eV down to the smallest allowed dark matter mass of about $10^{-21}$eV only a fraction of between 9\% to 54\% of the total neutrino mass can arise from the coupling to the background scalar, depending on the value of the scalar mass. Future data from JUNO may improve these limits down to 1\% in certain regions of scalar masses. We focus on a real scalar field, but most of our results hold also for a complex scalar.

</details>


### [89] [Axion Dark Matter Modulated Spin Wave Interferometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07136)
*Jiayi Liu,Chen-Hui Xie,Runyu Lei,Sichun Sun,Yu Gao,Jinxing Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新型非对称自旋波干涉仪（asymmetric spin wave interferometer），用于探测超轻轴子暗物质（ultralight axion dark matter）。该干涉仪利用轴子在铁磁体中产生的弱有效磁场调制自旋波性质，通过破坏性干涉将轴子诱导的相位偏移转换为可测量的磁化振荡，进而辐射电磁波并产生电信号。研究评估了三种探测方案的信噪比，可探测的轴子质量范围约为10⁻⁸ eV至10⁻⁶ eV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轴子（axion）是暗物质的候选粒子之一，超轻轴子的探测对理解暗物质本质具有重要意义。当前需要一种新的实验方案来突破现有探测手段的限制，实现对超轻轴子的有效探测。

Method: 提出一种非对称自旋波干涉仪：将自旋波源分为两条长度不同的路径，并设置破坏性干涉条件。轴子通过弱有效磁场调制自旋波特性，由轴子诱导的相位偏移被干涉仪转换为磁化振荡，该振荡可通过法拉第感应（Faraday induction）辐射电磁波并产生电信号。论文评估了线性放大器（linear amplifier）、单光子探测器（single-photon detector）和电信号探测三种方案的信噪比，同时计入磁化涨落和热噪声。

Result: 给出了三种探测方案（线性放大器、单光子探测器、电信号探测）的信噪比评估结果，均考虑了磁化涨落与热噪声的影响。确定了可探测轴子质量范围为约10⁻⁸ eV至10⁻⁶ eV，该范围由自旋波传播长度和弛豫时间设定。

Conclusion: 非对称自旋波干涉仪构成一种可行的超轻轴子暗物质探测新途径，能够覆盖10⁻⁸ eV至10⁻⁶ eV的轴子质量窗口，为暗物质实验探测提供了新的技术方案。

Abstract: We propose a novel asymmetric spin wave interferometer to detect ultralight axion dark matter. The axion modulates spin-wave properties via a weak effective magnetic field in ferromagnets. The interferometer splits a spin-wave source into two paths of different lengths and sets them to interfere destructively. The system then converts the axion-induced phase shift into a measurable magnetization oscillation that can radiate electromagnetic waves and generate electrical signals via Faraday induction. The signal-to-noise ratios have been evaluated for three detection schemes: the linear amplifier, the single-photon detector, and the electrical signal detection approach, accounting for both magnetization fluctuation and thermal noise. The accessible axion mass range is approximately $10^{-8}$ eV to $10^{-6}$ eV, set by the spin wave propagation length and the relaxation time.

</details>


### [90] [Chiral Doubling of Heavy-Light Hadrons and the New Beauty--Strange $B_{s0}^*(5700)^0$ Candidate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07268)
*Maciej A. Nowak,Ismail Zahed*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于LHCb合作组新观测到的美斯特介子（beauty-strange meson）Bs0*(5700)0，回顾了手征加倍（chiral doubling）理论框架，验证该新态在J^P=0^+射道下可作为Bs0手征伙伴（chiral partner），并预言一个尚未观测到的1^+美斯特介子位于5747±2 MeV处。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LHCb合作组新观测的Bs0*(5700)0介子为检验重轻子强子（heavy-light hadrons）的手征组织结构提供了新的重要测试机会，本文旨在通过理论回顾与对比验证手征加倍预言的正确性。

Method: 本文简要回顾手征加倍的对称性起源，通过重粒子手征有效理论（heavy-hadron chiral effective theory）推导普适的宇称劈裂（parity splitting），并将新LHCb观测与原始理论预言进行对比分析。

Result: 在J^P=0^+射道下，新观测的Bs0*(5700)0质量与作为B_s^0手征伙伴的解释一致；手征加倍框架进一步要求存在一个尚未观测到的窄宽度1^+美斯特介子，质量位于5747±2 MeV。

Conclusion: 若新美斯特态确认为J^P=0^+，其质量支持手征加倍理论预言，且该理论框架预示一个待观测的1^+美斯特介子，可作为后续实验验证的关键目标。

Abstract: The recent observation by the LHCb Collaboration of a new beauty--strange meson $B_{s0}^*(5700)^0$~\cite{LHCb:2026Bs0star}, under the conditional assignment $J^P=0^+$ provides an important new test of the chiral organization of heavy-light hadrons. More than three decades ago it was proposed that the coexistence of heavy-quark spin symmetry and spontaneously broken chiral symmetry implies that every heavy-light spin multiplet should possess an opposite-parity partner separated by a nearly universal mass gap determined primarily by the dynamics of the light degrees of freedom~\cite{Nowak:1993vc,Bardeen:1993ae}. This chiral doubling scenario was later developed into a quantitative heavy-hadron effective theory and extended to the complete charm and beauty spectra. In particular, the strange beauty sector was predicted to exhibit a parity splitting closely related to that of the strange charm sector, with only small $1/m_Q$ corrections. We briefly review the symmetry origin of chiral doubling, derive the universal parity splitting through the heavy-hadron chiral effective theory, and discuss the new LHCb observation in the context of the original theoretical predictions. Taken together with the established charm spectrum, if the new beauty-strange state is confirmed to have $J^P=0^+$, its mass is consistent with its interpretation as the chiral partner of the ground state $B_s^0$. Finally, we stress that the above confirmation of the spin and parity imposes in the chiral doubling scenario an existence of yet unobserved, very narrow beauty-strange meson with assignment $1^+$ at 5747 $\pm$ 2 MeV. This work is dedicated to the memory of our friend and collaborator Mannque Rho (1936--2026).

</details>

*Indra Kumar Banerjee,Ujjal Kumar Dey,Anna John*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究利用黑洞超辐射（superradiance）过程产生的暗光子，通过猝灭机制探测标准模型之外的中微子电磁矩（neutrino electromagnetic moments），并给出原初黑洞丰度的边界约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中微子可以在标准模型内通过高阶辐射修正获得电磁矩，也可以在新物理领域中产生。作者希望探索超出标准模型的中微子电磁矩。

Method: 考虑黑洞超辐射产生暗光子（dark photon），暗光子再通过中微子-反中微子对的产生实现猝灭（quenching）。对产生效率取决于暗光子与中微子之间的有效耦合，即暗电磁矩（dark electromagnetic moments）。

Result: 给出由该猝灭超辐射机制产生的中微子背景对原初黑洞丰度（primordial black hole abundance）的约束边界。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一种通过黑洞超辐射和中微子背景观测来探测超出标准模型的中微子电磁矩的新途径。

Abstract: Neutrinos can acquire electromagnetic moments either within the Standard Model through higher order radiative corrections or within the domain of new physics. In this study we focus on probing these beyond the standard model neutrino moments through quenched superradiance of black holes where fermionic pairs can be produced from the superradiant bosonic cloud. We consider the production of dark photons from black hole superradiance and quenching occurs through the production of neutrino-antineutrino pairs from the dark photons. The efficiency of the pair production depends on the effective coupling between the dark photons and neutrinos, i.e., the dark electromagnetic moments. We also discuss bounds on primordial black hole abundance from neutrino background arising from this quenched superradiance mechanism.

</details>

### [92] [Sublattice-resolved coherent phonon dynamics in charge density waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06456)
*Kyoung Hun Oh,Honglie Ning,Zongqi Shen,Yifan Su,Jack Maier,Gyeongbo Kang,Hyeongi Choi,Dong Wu,Qiaomei Liu,Hyun-Woo J. Kim,Seunghyeok Ha,Jaehwon Kim,Byungjune Lee,B. J. Kim,N. L. Wang,Yao Wang,Hoyoung Jang,Nuh Gedik*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 声子（phonon）决定材料基本性质并在电子相变中起到核心作用，通过相干驱动特定声子模式可实现按需的相控制，这要求对实空间声子运动进行亚晶格分辨的识别，但实验上解析这些运动仍具挑战性，限制了基于声子的精确控制。

Method: 利用时间分辨共振X射线散射的元素选择性，对电荷密度波材料EuTe4中的相干声子动力学进行追踪；通过该时域方法揭示Eu与Te亚晶格主导的三种相干声子模式，并与声子本征矢的理论计算进行对比验证。

Result: 在EuTe4中发现主流的Te亚晶格电荷序伴随有此前未被报道的Eu亚晶格组分，并成功解耦出Eu与Te主导的晶格动力学，实验结果与理论计算的声子本征矢吻合良好。

Conclusion: 该时域方法超越了传统频域非弹性散射的能量分辨率限制，为在多元素材料中分解相干声子提供了广泛适用的框架，对物质的靶向相控制具有关键意义。

Abstract: Phonons govern fundamental material properties and play a central role in various electronic phase transitions. Coherent driving of specific phonon modes enables on-demand phase control, motivating sublattice-resolved identification of real-space phonon motions. Yet experimentally resolving these motions remains challenging, limiting precise phonon-based control. Here, we introduce a dynamical protocol to track element-resolved phonon dynamics in the charge density wave material EuTe4, in which the dominant Te-sublattice charge order is accompanied by a previously unreported Eu-sublattice component. We leverage the elemental selectivity of time-resolved resonant X-ray scattering to reveal three coherent phonon modes with distinct sublattice character, thereby disentangling Eu- and Te-dominated lattice dynamics, in good agreement with theoretical calculations of the phonon eigenvectors. This time-domain approach, which surpasses the energy-resolution limits of conventional frequency-domain inelastic scattering, provides a broadly applicable framework for decomposing coherent phonons in multi-element materials, which is crucial for the targeted control of phases of matter.

</details>


### [93] [Nonreciprocity reversal of magnetoacoustic attenuation in NiFe alloy thin films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06568)
*Mingran Xu,Kei Yamamoto,Kouta Kondou,Zheng Zhu,Liyang Liao,Kiyohiro Adachi,Tomoka Kikitsu,Daisuke Hashizume,Dirk Grundler,Sadamichi Maekawa,Yoshichika Otani*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文在Ni_xFe_{100-x}薄膜中观测到非互易性（nonreciprocity）符号反转现象，发现通过在坡莫合金（Permalloy）的零磁致伸缩组分附近仅改变0.8 at.%成分，即可使磁弹性系数b改变符号，从而反转椭球有效场的旋向性并使非互易性从78.6%变为-61.8%。这一发现将非互易性确立为探测纳米尺度磁弹性耦合的有效手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非互易性是二极管、微波隔离器等器件的基础物理机制。在铁磁体中，表面声波会产生具有传播锁定旋向性的椭球有效场，导致传播的非互易性，但数十年来一直缺乏控制非互易性符号的方法，这是该领域亟待解决的问题。

Method: 采用角度依赖测量（angle-dependent measurements）和自旋波椭率建模（spin-wave-ellipticity modelling），系统研究Ni_xFe_{100-x}薄膜中磁弹性系数b符号变化对非互易性的影响，并结合立方频率标度（cubic frequency scaling）方法在10纳米薄膜中解析b的符号。

Result: 实验观测到非互易性符号反转：在坡莫合金零磁致伸缩组分附近仅0.8 at.%的成分变化即令b改变符号，非互易性从78.6%反转为-61.8%。角度依赖测量和建模证实b符号反转导致椭球偏振有效场旋向性反转。在10纳米薄膜中可分辨b低至-0.05 MPa的符号。

Conclusion: 该研究首次实现了对非互易性符号的主动控制，建立了成分-磁弹性耦合-非互易性符号之间的直接联系，并将非互易性确立为纳米尺度磁弹性耦合的探测工具，为相关器件设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Nonreciprocity, the asymmetry of transport, underlies technologies from the diode to the microwave isolator. In a ferromagnet, a surface acoustic wave generates an elliptical effective field with propagation-locked handedness, breaking the reciprocity of its propagation. Despite decades of study on this phenomenon, a method for controlling the sign of the nonreciprocity has remained elusive. Here we observe a sign reversal in Ni$_x$Fe$_{100-x}$ films. A 0.8 at.% change across Permalloy's zero-magnetostriction composition, where the magnetoelastic coefficient $b$ changes sign, reverses the handedness of the elliptical effective field and thereby the nonreciprocity, from 78.6% to -61.8%. Angle-dependent measurements and spin-wave-ellipticity modelling show that reversing the sign of $b$ reverses the handedness of the elliptically polarized effective field. Aided by cubic frequency scaling, we resolve the sign of $b$ down to -0.05 MPa in a 10-nm film, establishing nonreciprocity as a nanoscale probe of magnetoelastic coupling.

</details>


### [94] [Extracting the full conductivity tensor in a rectangular sample](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06643)
*Julia Gelfond,Oskar Vafek*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种简单实验流程，通过共形映射（conformal mapping）解析方法获得矩形几何二维材料上点源与漏极间的电势表达式，从而提取完整的电导率张量（conductivity tensor），包括主轴角度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多二维材料的电输运测量显示各向异性电导率，但现有矩形几何方法只能提取部分张量分量或实验难以执行，因此需要一种能提取完整电导率张量的简单流程。

Method: 采用共形映射方法推导矩形几何上点源和漏极的电势解析表达式，进而获取完整的电导率张量（或通过求逆获得电阻率张量），包括主轴角度。有限尺寸的源/漏可通过叠加原理建模。

Result: 解析解与COMSOL数值模拟以及主轴角度为零的已知极限情形吻合良好，验证了方法的正确性。

Conclusion: 所提出的实验流程能简单且准确地提取二维材料完整的电导率张量及其主轴角度，为各向异性二维材料的电输运表征提供了可行性方案。

Abstract: Electrical transport measurements reveal that many 2D materials exhibit anisotropic conductivity. However, current methodologies for rectangular geometries can only extract a partial conductivity tensor or are difficult to execute experimentally. Here, we propose a simple experimental procedure to extract the full conductivity tensor (or the resistivity tensor by inversion) including the principal axes angle. Our procedure is developed by using a conformal mapping approach to obtain an analytical expression for the potential with a point source and drain on the perimeter. Our solution agrees very well with numerical simulations using COMSOL and the known limiting case where the principal axes angle vanishes. Finite source/drains can be modeled using superposition.

</details>


### [95] [Cross-Geometry Transferability Assessment of Universal Machine Learning Interatomic Potentials: From Bulk Materials to Atomic Nanowires](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06662)
*Pedro H. M. Zanineli,Bruno Focassio,Gabriel R. Schleder*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究构建了涵盖本体、表面、颗粒、颈部及原子线等多种结构几何环境的ZrO2密度泛函理论数据集，系统评估了26个预训练机器学习原子间势（MLIP）在零样本预测、微调和从零训练三种策略下的表现，发现基于平均能量和力误差的排名无法普遍预测物理性质层面的行为，表明几何多样化的目标数据与独立的物理验证对适配低配位离子纳米结构至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础机器学习原子间势（foundation MLIPs）能以远低于第一性原理方法的计算成本进行原子级模拟，但其在不同结构几何环境下的可靠性尚未得到充分理解，尤其对于低配位离子纳米结构缺乏系统性评估。

Method: 构建了基于密度泛函理论（density functional theory）的ZrO2构型数据集，涵盖体相、表面、颗粒、颈部（neck）和原子线（atomic wire）等环境；对26个预训练MLIP进行基准测试，并系统比较零样本推断、微调和从零训练三种适配策略，同时评估弹性、振动、表面能和颈部动力学等物理性质。

Result: 零样本预测存在显著的几何依赖退化；对齐参考能量后最优零样本模型ORB-V3达到6 meV/atom的能量误差和197.3 meV/Å的力误差，其中颈部和原子线构型的力误差最大；微调效果优于从零训练且耗时相当；几何特异性微调提升域内精度却对其他结构产生负迁移，混合几何微调可减少跨几何误差；基于平均能量和力误差的排名不能普适预测性质级行为。

Conclusion: 适配基础机器学习原子间势到低配位离子纳米结构时，必须引入几何多样化的目标数据和独立的物理性质验证，不能仅依赖平均能力和力误差指标进行模型选择。

Abstract: Foundation machine-learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) enable atomistic simulations at substantially lower computational cost than first-principles methods, but their reliability across structural geometries remains insufficiently understood. Here, we construct a density-functional-theory dataset of ZrO2 configurations spanning bulk, slab, particle, neck, and atomically thin wire environments motivated by an experimentally observed ZrO2 desintering process involving neck thinning and atomic wire formation. We first benchmark 26 pretrained MLIPs and observe pronounced geometry-dependent degradation in zero-shot predictions. Without any training, after only reference-energy alignment, the best zero-shot model (ORB-V3) reaches energy and force root-mean-square errors of 6 meV/atom and 197.3 meV/Å, respectively, with the largest force errors in neck and wire configurations. We then compare zero-shot inference, fine-tuning, and training from scratch strategies. Fine-tuning yields lower energy and force errors than training from scratch, while both require comparable wall-clock time. Geometry-specific fine-tuning improves in-domain accuracy but frequently produces negative transfer to other structural classes, whereas mixed-geometry fine-tuning reduces cross-geometry errors. Evaluations of elastic and vibrational properties, surface energies, and neck dynamics further show that rankings based on average energy and force errors do not universally predict property-level behavior. These results demonstrate that geometry-diverse target data and independent physical validations are necessary when adapting foundation MLIPs to low-coordination (ionic) nanostructures.

</details>


### [96] [Universal Magnetoresistance Scaling in Layered Pd-based Multiband Metals Beyond Compensated Semimetal Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06700)
*Kenjiro Okawa,Takao Sasagawa*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了单晶非磁性层状Pd基金属（包括中心对称的PdTe2、PdPb2、β-PdBi2以及非中心对称的α-PdBi）的磁电阻比（magnetoresistance ratio, MR），识别出一类位于多带、高载流子密度体系中的大磁电阻，并发现其MR遵循由载流子迁移率决定的简单标度关系。与Dirac和Weyl半金属中由简单补偿载流子模型描述的超大MR不同，这些化合物具有复杂费米面（Fermi surface），但在高残余电阻率比（residual resistivity ratio, RRR）晶体中仍满足Kohler法则，表明MR由单一有效散射时间控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期以来，极端大磁电阻（extremely large MR）现象主要集中于Dirac和Weyl半金属等由简单补偿载流子模型描述的体系中，而对于具有复杂费米面和多带结构的常规高载流子密度金属，其大磁电阻的物理机制尚缺乏系统认识。本文旨在探索非磁性金属材料中是否也存在一类可归类的大磁电阻现象，并揭示其微观物理机制。

Method: 本研究对四种Pd基金属单晶（中心对称的PdTe2、PdPb2、β-PdBi2以及非中心对称的α-PdBi）进行了系统的磁电阻测量，并结合第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）验证其电子结构和费米面复杂性。通过考察磁电阻对磁场、温度以及残余电阻率比的依赖关系，分析了标度行为和Kohler法则的适用性，并对中心对称与非中心对称体系进行了等RRR基础上的对比分析。

Result: 研究发现这些化合物尽管具有复杂费米面，但其磁电阻仍展示由载流子迁移率决定的简单标度，且在高RRR晶体中Kohler法则成立。磁电阻的场和RRR依赖呈介于线性和二次之间的中间幂律行为，归因于载流子不完全补偿和迁移率分布。α-PdBi因极高RRR（约660）在2 K、7 T下达到最大MR 1500%，但在等RRR比较下其MR小于中心对称对应物，表明非中心对称体系中自旋-轨道诱导的能带分裂带来的额外散射通道降低了有效载流子迁移率。

Conclusion: 本工作确立了一类新的洁净多带金属中的大磁电阻，其中复杂电子结构产生了涌现的单参数标度（emergent single-parameter scaling），凸显了无序、迁移率与对称性之间的相互制约。该发现拓展了大磁电阻材料的分类范围，并为理解多带体系中磁电阻的普适规律提供了新的视角。

Abstract: We systematically investigated the magnetoresistance ratio (MR) of single-crystalline, nonmagnetic layered Pd-based metals, including centrosymmetric PdTe2, PdPb2, and beta-PdBi2 and noncentrosymmetric alpha-PdBi. Our study identifies a distinct class of large MR in multiband, high-carrier-density systems. Unlike well-studied extremely large MR materials, such as Dirac and Weyl semimetals described by simple compensated-carrier models, these compounds possess complex Fermi surfaces, as validated by our first-principles calculations. Nevertheless, they exhibit a remarkably simple MR scaling governed by carrier mobility, manifested in systematic dependencies on magnetic field, temperature, and the residual resistivity ratio (RRR). The validity of Kohler's rule in high-RRR crystals indicates that MR is governed by a single effective scattering time, even in these multiband systems. The field and RRR dependences of MR follow an intermediate power-law behavior between linear and quadratic, attributable to imperfect carrier compensation and a distribution of carrier mobilities. Among the studied compounds, alpha-PdBi exhibits the largest MR, reaching 1500% (2 K, 7 T), owing to its exceptionally high RRR (approximately 660). However, when compared on an equal-RRR basis, its MR is smaller than that of its centrosymmetric counterparts. This trend suggests that additional scattering channels arising from spin-orbit-induced band splitting in noncentrosymmetric systems reduce the effective carrier mobility. Our results establish a new class of large MR in clean multiband metals where complex electronic structures give rise to emergent single-parameter scaling, highlighting the interplay between disorder, mobility, and symmetry.

</details>


### [97] [Giant-exchange-driven Vectorial Control of a Minimal Topological Magnet in Eu3In2As4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06737)
*Haonan Chen,Xunkai Duan,Guangyi Wang,Yuhan Du,Huayao Li,Jiayu Wang,Wenbin Wu,Zixuan Xu,Yingchao Xia,Jiaming Gu,Pengliang Leng,Lin Miao,Fengfeng Zhu,Xiang Yuan,Tong Zhou,Cheng Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁性与能带拓扑之间的相互作用为控制量子物态提供了途径，但在实际材料中常受限于弱交换耦合和复杂电子结构，因此需要寻找具有强交换耦合的模型材料来系统研究磁场调控拓扑相变。

Method: 通过理论预测与第一性原理计算识别Eu3In2As4中的巨交换耦合，结合量子振荡、反常霍尔输运和磁红外光谱等实验手段，测量交换驱动的能带重构，并在理论上分析磁化旋转对Weyl节点动量空间位置和分离度的调控效果。

Result: Eu3In2As4中巨交换耦合产生高达300 meV的磁化依赖带位移；在温和磁场下，反铁磁拓扑绝缘体基态依次转变为2/3铁磁中间相和完全极化的铁磁态，后者被预测为单对Weyl节点或节点环半金属；量子振荡、反常霍尔输运和磁红外光谱一致揭示了这些转变中的能带重构。

Conclusion: Eu3In2As4被确立为研究强交换耦合如何以最小复杂度控制拓扑能带结构的模型体系，磁化旋转为调节Weyl节点在动量空间中的位置和分离度提供了高效手段。

Abstract: The interplay between magnetism and band topology provides a route to controlling quantum states of matter, yet its realization in materials is often constrained by weak exchange coupling and complex electronic structures. Here, a giant exchange coupling is identified in the newly predicted topological magnet Eu3In2As4, giving rise to magnetization-dependent band shifts of up to 300 meV. Together with its intrinsically soft magnetic response, this strong cou-pling enables systematic tuning of topological phases by both the magnitude and orientation of applied magnetic fields. The magneto-topological phase diagram is mapped out in which an antiferromagnetic topological insulator ground state evolves, under modest fields, into a pro-posed intermediate 2/3-ferrimagnetic phase, and further into fully polarized ferromagnetic states predicted to host either Weyl or nodal-ring semimetals. Notably, the Weyl phase corresponds to a minimal model hosting a single pair of Weyl nodes. Quantum oscillations, anomalous Hall transport and magneto-infrared spectroscopy consistently reveal exchange-driven band recon-struction across these transitions. Rotation of the magnetization theoretically provides an effi-cient means to tune the momentum-space positions and separations of the Weyl nodes. These results establish Eu3In2As4 as a model system for exploring how strong exchange coupling can be used to control topological band structures with minimal complexity.

</details>


### [98] [Optical Anisotropy and Phase Matching in Non-Centrosymmetric Perovskite Oxides from DFT+U and DFT+U+V Functionals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06759)
*Mohamed S. M. M. Ali,Ismaila Dabo*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文系统检验了 DFT+U 和 DFT+U+V 两类密度泛函近似在预测非中心对称钙钛矿氧化物（如 BaTiO₃、LiNbO₃、KNbO₃ 和 PbTiO₃）各向异性光学响应方面的精度，发现仅靠位内 Hubbard 修正会抑制光学各向异性，而引入位间 Hubbard 相互作用并合理选择（原子间）轨道流形后，DFT+U+V 能以远低于杂化泛函和多体微扰论的计算成本获得准确预测，并在 Zn 和 (Bi,Mn) 掺杂 BaTiO₃ 固溶体中展示出增强二色比和双折射的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光学各向异性（optical anisotropy）支撑着众多光子与量子技术的运行和性能，但现有密度泛函理论（DFT）近似对其预测的准确性尚未被严格检验，尤其对于非中心对称的钙钛矿氧化物材料。

Method: 作者系统评估了含位内 Hubbard 修正的 DFT+U 泛函与同时含位内和位间 Hubbard 修正的 DFT+U+V 泛函在预测 BaTiO₃、LiNbO₃、KNbO₃ 和 PbTiO₃ 各向异性光学响应时的准确性，并系统研究了定义 Hubbard 修正的（原子间）轨道流形选择对结果的影响。

Result: 仅使用 DFT+U 修正自相互作用误差并不能正确捕获这些材料的光电响应，反而常导致光学各向异性被抑制；引入位间 Hubbard 相互作用可恢复各向异性，但轨道流形的选择对精度至关重要。经验证后的 DFT+U+V 泛函在远低于杂化泛函和多体微扰论的计算成本下实现了相当的预测性能。在 Zn 和 (Bi,Mn) 取代的 BaTiO₃ 固溶体基准测试中，(Bi,Mn) 取代体系通过带隙中部态（mid-gap states）产生偏振依赖的带隙变窄，显著增强了二色比（dichroic ratio）和双折射（birefringence）。

Conclusion: 系统验证的 DFT+U+V 泛函为高效、准确地预测非中心对称钙钛矿氧化物及各向异性光学响应提供了可行方案，其在偏振敏感光电探测器和集成光子学等领域具有重要的应用前景。

Abstract: Optical anisotropy underpins the operation and performance of a broad range of photonic and quantum technologies. In this work, we critically examine the accuracy of density functional theory approximations with onsite and intersite Hubbard corrections (the DFT+$U$ and DFT+$U$+$V$ functionals) in predicting the anisotropic optical response of the non-centrosymmetric perovskite oxides, such as BaTiO$_3$, LiNbO$_3$, KNbO$_3$, and PbTiO$_3$. It is found that correcting self-interaction errors using DFT+$U$ alone does not capture the optoelectronic response of these materials, often leading to a suppression of their optical anisotropy. While intersite Hubbard interactions restore this anisotropy, the choice of the (inter)atomic orbital manifold that defines the Hubbard correction remains critical to its accuracy. The predictive performance of the resulting, systematically validated DFT+$U$+$V$ functional is achieved at a fraction of the computational cost of hybrid functionals and many-body perturbation theory calculations. As benchmarks, we investigate Zn- and (Bi,Mn)-substituted BaTiO$_3$ solid solutions; the latter exhibit polarization-dependent bandgap narrowing from mid-gap states, substantially enhancing the dichroic ratio and birefringence with promising implications for polarization-sensitive photodetectors and integrated photonics.

</details>


### [99] [Symmetry Classification of Non-Relativistic Hidden Spin Polarization in Noncollinear Magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06787)
*Yuzhong Hu,Pan Zhou,Baoru Pan,PengBo Lyu,Lizhong Sun*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文基于自旋群理论（spin-group theory）为非常线磁体（noncollinear magnets）中的隐藏自旋极化（hidden spin polarization, HSP）建立了一个统一对称性框架，将HSP分类为三类（HSP-1、HSP-2和HSP-3）并识别出四种分裂自旋织构（split spin-texture, SST）类型。作者通过紧束缚模型、代表性材料实例和MAGNDATA数据库调查验证了该框架，并在多种材料中发现了非零自旋相关响应张量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐藏自旋极化已在非磁性和共线磁性系统中被广泛研究，但在非常线磁体中尚缺乏系统性理解。该研究旨在填补这一空白，为非常线磁体中的自旋极化行为建立统一的理论框架，以促进自旋相关功能材料的设计与筛选。

Method: 作者基于自旋群理论建立统一对称性框架，通过分析自旋对称性对非相对论自旋极化的系统性约束，推导出每个局部扇区（local sector）的四种分裂自旋织构类型（SST-1至SST-4）；再结合局部自旋织构的维度和不同局部扇区之间的对称性关系，将HSP进一步分类为HSP-1、HSP-2和HSP-3。该方法通过紧束缚模型、代表性材料（如SrFe₂Se₂O、USb等）以及MAGNDATA数据库的整体调查来验证和推广。

Result: 研究确立了非常线磁体中HSP的统一对称性分类体系，识别出四种SST类型和三类HSP。MAGNDATA数据库调查发现133个、7个和139个候选材料分别对应HSP-1、HSP-2和HSP-3。对称性分析与第一性原理计算进一步表明，其中许多材料可以展现非零自旋相关响应张量。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解非常线磁体中的隐藏自旋极化建立了一般性框架，揭示了非常线磁性材料在自旋依赖功能（spin-dependent functionalities）方面的潜在应用前景，为后续材料筛选和器件设计提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Hidden spin polarization (HSP), in which spin-polarized states exist locally while the total spin polarization are hidden in momentum space, has been extensively studied in nonmagnetic and collinear magnetic systems, but remains largely unexplored in noncollinear magnets. Here we establish a unified symmetry framework for HSP in noncollinear magnetic materials based on spin-group theory. We show that spin symmetries systematically constrain nonrelativistic spin polarization, giving rise to four distinct split spin-texture (SST) types for each local sector, denoted as SST-1, SST-2, SST-3, and SST-4. Based on these splitting forms, together with the dimensionality of the associated local spin textures and the symmetry relations between different local sectors, we further classify HSP into three categories: HSP-1, HSP-2, and HSP-3. We illustrate these categories using tight-binding models and representative material examples, including SrFe$_2$Se$_2$O, USb, Sr$_2$Mn$_3$Sb$_2$O$_2$, PrFeAsO, and GdMn$_2$Si$_2$. A survey of the MAGNDATA database further identifies 133, 7, and 139 candidate noncollinear magnetic materials hosting HSP-1, HSP-2, and HSP-3, respectively. In addition, our symmetry analysis and first-principles calculation show that many of these materials can exhibit nonzero spin-related response tensors. These results establish a general framework for understanding HSP in noncollinear magnets and highlight their potential for spin-dependent functionalities.

</details>


### [100] [Autonomous Optimization of Complex Oxides for Thermochemical Fuel Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06877)
*Shuiping Gong,Mingcheng Li,Han Hao,Zhenhao Zhou,Yi Li,Xiaobo Liao,Cheng Fang,Jian Deng,Jiangang He,Wenpei Gao,Yakun Yuan,Chris Wolverton,Tao Deng,Chaochao Dun,Runxia Cai,Zhenpeng Yao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文是一篇综述论文，论证了热化学燃料生产（thermochemical fuel production）作为自主材料设计与优化前沿领域的必要性，系统梳理了复杂氧化物（complex oxides）从合成、表征、测试到计算与多目标优化的能力路线图，并指出了构建自改进材料开发平台所面临的关键挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两步热化学燃料生产需要同时满足多个严格的热力学与动力学目标，驱动材料设计向多阳离子复杂氧化物方向发展，但传统的试错方法难以应对多维相空间、苛刻操作条件与相互竞争的功能目标所构成的复杂材料优化问题。

Method: 采用综述性方法，批判性分析复杂氧化物开发的难点，系统整合高通量计算（high-throughput computation）、自动合成、表征与测试以及机器学习（machine learning）等工具，并将这些工具组织为闭环工作流（closed-loop workflows）和复杂氧化物优化能力路线图。

Result: 提出了覆盖成分多样性合成、操作状态表征（operando characterization）、机器人测试（robotic testing）、操作条件计算和多目标优化（multi-objective optimization）的复杂氧化物优化能力路线图，并识别出构建自改进材料开发平台面临的实验、计算和数据三方面关键挑战。

Conclusion: 热化学燃料生产为自主材料设计与优化提供了令人信服的前沿应用场景，但实现自改进型材料开发平台仍需克服多种跨学科挑战，涉及实验、计算与数据基础设施的协同发展。

Abstract: Two-step thermochemical fuel production, including H2O and CO2 splitting, offers a promising route to sustainable fuel manufacturing, with performance governed by redox-active oxides that enable cyclic reduction-oxidation reactions. Maximizing thermal-to-fuel conversion efficiency demands materials that simultaneously satisfy multiple stringent thermodynamic and kinetic targets. Addressing these requirements has increasingly driven materials design toward complex, multi-cation oxides, such as mixed-cation fluorites, perovskites, and high-entropy oxides, wherein composition, defect chemistry, phase stability, and morphology should be co-optimized. This creates a challenging materials optimization problem that is poorly suited to traditional trial-and-error approaches. In this review, we argue that thermochemical fuel production provides a compelling frontier for autonomous materials design and optimization. We first examine why redox-active complex oxides are difficult to develop, owing to multidimensional phase spaces, harsh operating conditions, and competing functional targets. We then discuss how high-throughput computation, automated synthesis, characterization and testing, and machine learning can be integrated into closed-loop workflows to address these challenges. Building on broader oxide materials research, we organize recent progress into a capability roadmap for complex-oxide optimization, spanning compositionally diverse synthesis, operando characterization, robotic testing, operation-condition computation, and multi-objective optimization. Finally, we outline key experimental, computational, and data challenges for building self-improving materials development platforms for materials development in thermochemical fuel production.

</details>


### [101] [Local Structure Dictates Ionic Transport and Mechanical Properties in Glassy Solid Electrolytes for Lithium Batteries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06895)
*Yong Li,Tao Du,Rasmus Christensen,Timothée Jamin,Zhencai Li,Qi Zhang,Xiaoyi Xu,Kasper Tolborg,Yuanzheng Yue,Morten M. Smedskjaer*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过基于机器学习原子间势（machine learning interatomic potential）的分子动力学模拟，揭示了玻璃态Li-S-P-B-I（LSPBI）固态电解质中B-S与P-S网络的相互作用如何调控离子电导率与力学形变行为，发现适度的P2S5含量可通过解聚刚性硼骨架同时提升离子输运与纳米延展性，而过量P2S5则因多聚磷酸盐形成反而阻碍Li+迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硫化物和卤化物玻璃是固态锂电池（all-solid-state lithium batteries）的候选电解质，具备可加工性、无晶界和较高离子电导率等优势，但其离子电导率和力学性能仍不足以满足实际应用需求，需要深入理解其微观结构以推动性能突破。

Method: 采用基于机器学习原子间势的分子动力学模拟（molecular dynamics simulations），对一系列玻璃态固态电解质进行结构与性能关联研究，系统考察B-S与P-S网络在玻璃态Li-S-P-B-I中的相互影响及其对离子电导率和形变行为的调控作用。

Result: 向B2S3基玻璃中引入P2S5可触发关键结构转变，同时增强离子电导率和力学纳米延展性；适中的P2S5含量下，PS4单元解聚刚性硼骨架并形成快速离子输运的渗流扩散路径，同时灵活的P-S-P构型通过键弯曲实现能量耗散，引发脆-延转变；而过量P2S5则增加多聚磷酸盐（如P2S6和P2S7）比例，使结构网络聚合化并阻碍Li+迁移。

Conclusion: 该工作为设计具有离子电导率与力学鲁棒性平衡的玻璃电解质提供了原子尺度设计原则，揭示结构网络拓扑与性能之间的内在关联。

Abstract: Electrolytes composed of sulfide and halide glasses are promising candidates for all-solid-state lithium batteries owing to their processability, lack of grain boundaries, and relatively high ionic conductivity. Nevertheless, their ionic conductivity and mechanical properties are still not satisfying for the real-world applications. Significant advances in solid electrolytes require a thorough understanding of their microstructures. Here, we reveal the connections among structure, ionic transport properties, and mechanical stability in a series of glassy solid electrolytes by employing molecular dynamics simulations based on a machine learning interatomic potential. Specifically, we explore how the interplay between B-S and P-S networks in glassy Li-S-P-B-I (LSPBI) governs ionic conductivity and deformation behavior. The introduction of P2S5 into a B2S3-based glass induces a critical structural transformation, through which both ionic conductivity and mechanical nano-ductility can be enhanced. For a moderate P2S5 content, incorporated PS4 units depolymerize the rigid boron framework, creating percolative diffusion pathways for fast ionic transport. Concurrently, the flexible P-S-P configurations enable energy dissipation through bond bending, leading to the brittle-to-ductile transition. However, excessive P2S5 increases the fraction of polyphosphates (e.g., P2S6 and P2S7), thereby polymerizing the structural network and ultimately impeding Li+ mobility. Our work thus provides atomistic principles for engineering glass electrolytes with balanced ionic conductivity and mechanical robustness.

</details>


### [102] [Fast Isotropic Li-Ion Diffusion in Zeolitic Imidazolate Framework Glass Electrolytes for Batteries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06902)
*Yong Li,Tao Du,Timothée Jamin,Zhencai Li,Kasper Tolborg,Yuanzheng Yue,Morten M. Smedskjaer*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过机器学习原子间势（machine learning interatomic potential）模拟研究了ZIF-4和ZIF-62两种沸石咪唑酯骨架（ZIF）玻璃中的锂离子输运行为，发现结构无序可显著降低锂离子迁移活化能（从约0.35 eV降至0.16 eV）并大幅提升室温扩散系数（ZIF-4提高一个多数量级，ZIF-62提高近七倍），同时使原本高度各向异性的扩散转变为各向同性。该研究表明金属有机框架（MOF）玻璃是极具潜力的高性能固态电解质材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全固态锂电池需要同时具备快速室温离子输运、力学稳健性和良好界面相容性的固态电解质。ZIF玻璃作为金属有机框架（metal-organic framework, MOF）的一个亚类，兼具无晶界（grain boundary-free）的非晶拓扑结构与化学可调框架，是极具吸引力但尚未充分探索的材料平台，有必要系统揭示其离子输运机理。

Method: 利用机器学习原子间势对晶体态和玻璃态的ZIF-4及ZIF-62进行分子动力学模拟，计算锂离子输运的活化能和扩散系数；通过非高斯动力学（non-Gaussian dynamics）分析和范霍夫相关函数（van Hove correlation functions）解析锂离子扩散的微观机理与动态异质性。

Result: 结构无序将锂离子迁移活化能从约0.35 eV降低至0.16 eV，并使外推的室温扩散系数在ZIF-4中提高一个多数量级、在ZIF-62中提高近七倍。晶体态ZIF中锂离子扩散呈现强烈各向异性，源于咪唑盐和苯并咪唑盐环的有序取向在不同晶向上施加不同能垒；玻璃化（vitrification）后环取向随机化，扩散变为各向同性或近各向同性。此外，晶体态中锂离子扩散通过稀有且动态异质的跃迁事件在明确笼间进行，而玻璃态中扩散更均匀、连续且近似菲克（Fickian-like）行为。

Conclusion: 结构无序是解锁ZIF玻璃中快速各向同性锂离子扩散的关键机制，设计良好的MOF玻璃有望成为高性能固态电解质的有力候选材料。

Abstract: All-solid-state lithium batteries require solid electrolytes that combine rapid room-temperature ion transport with mechanical robustness and interfacial compatibility. Zeolitic imidazolate framework (ZIF) glasses, with ZIFs being a sub-set of metal-organic frameworks, offer an attractive yet relatively underexplored platform because they combine an grainboundary-free and amorphous topology with chemically tunable frameworks. Here, we reveal that structural disorder unlocks fast and isotropic lithium diffusion in ZIF glasses. This is realized by using a machine learning interatomic potential to simulate Li+ transport in crystalline and glassy ZIF-4 and ZIF-62. Structural disorder reduces the activation energy for Li+ migration from ~0.35 eV to 0.16 eV and increases the extrapolated room-temperature diffusion coefficient by more than one order of magnitude for ZIF-4 and nearly sevenfold for ZIF-62. Analyses of non-Gaussian dynamics and van Hove correlation functions reveal that Li+ diffusion in crystalline ZIFs occurs via rare, dynamically heterogeneous hopping events among well-defined cages, whereas Li+ diffusion in glassy ZIFs is more homogeneous, continuous, and Fickian-like, benefiting from a wide distribution of coordination geometries and migration barriers. Li+ diffusion in crystalline ZIFs is strongly anisotropic, reflecting that ordered orientations of imidazolate and benzimidazolate rings impose distinct energy barriers along different crystallographic directions. Upon vitrification, these ring orientations become randomized, and hence, the diffusion of Li+ becomes isotropic or near-isotropic. These findings imply that well-designed metal-organic framework glasses are a promising candidate as high-performance solid-state electrolytes.

</details>


### [103] [Spin-group theory on Edelstein effect and spin-orbit torque in Collinear Ferromagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06964)
*Yizhuo Song,Qing Zhang,Jiahao Shentu,Jie Li,Jia Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文发展了用于共线铁磁体中Edelstein效应和自旋轨道力矩（SOT）的自旋群对称性理论，将自旋轨道耦合（SOC）视为对称性破缺扰动，系统推导了不同点群对称性下类场和类阻尼力矩的完整形式，揭示了不同阶次SOC的贡献规律，并通过第一性原理计算验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过渡金属/共线铁磁体双层结构中，类场力矩和类阻尼力矩此前的描述仅停留在唯象层面，依赖自旋或轨道霍尔效应，缺乏严格的对称性基础；同时自旋轨道耦合在Edelstein效应和SOT中的精确作用关系尚未阐明。

Method: 作者建立了适用于共线铁磁体中Edelstein效应和SOT的自旋群（spin group）对称性理论，将自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling, SOC）作为对称性破缺微扰处理，并针对4mm（C4v）和3m（C3v）等点群对称性推导类场力矩和类阻尼力矩的完整形式。

Result: 研究发现：在4mm对称性下，类场和类阻尼力矩分别主要来自一阶和二阶SOC；轨道霍尔主导的Ti/Ni和自旋霍尔主导的Pt/CoFe双层结构中的SOT均起源于一阶SOC；在PtMnSb中，特定点群对称性下零阶和一阶SOC贡献完全消失，领先SOT项出现在二阶SOC。

Conclusion: 所有基于自旋群理论的对称性预测与第一性原理计算结果高度定量一致，该工作为铁磁体系中Edelstein效应和电流诱导自旋力矩的微观理解建立了统一的对称性框架。

Abstract: Current-induced spin-orbit torques (SOTs) are central to the electrical manipulation of magnetic order in spintronic devices. In transition-metal/collinear ferromagnet bilayers, field-like and damping-like torques have been described only phenomenologically via the spin or orbital Hall effect, lacking a rigorous symmetry-based foundation. The precise role of spin-orbit coupling (SOC) in both the Edelstein effect and SOTs has remained unresolved. Here we develop a spin-group symmetry theory for the Edelstein effect and SOTs in collinear ferromagnets, treating SOC as a symmetry-breaking perturbation. For 4mm (C4v) point group symmetry, we derive the full forms of field-like and damping-like torques, which arise predominantly from first- and second-order SOC. We further show that SOTs in both orbital-Hall-dominated Ti/Ni and spin-Hall-dominated Pt/CoFe bilayers originate at first-order SOC. Taking the 3m (C3v) torque as a paradigmatic example, we elucidate the role of second- and higher-order SOC torques in field-free switching of perpendicular magnetic anisotropy. Remarkably, in PtMnSb, we demonstrate that SOTs under certain point group symmetries deviate from the conventional form: zeroth- and first-order SOC contributions vanish identically, with the leading SOT emerging at second order. All symmetry-based predictions from spin-group theory are in excellent quantitative agreement with first-principles calculations. Our work establishes a unified symmetry framework for the microscopic understanding of the Edelstein effect and current-induced spin torques in ferromagnetic systems.

</details>


### [104] [Local lattice dynamics of hcp zinc from EXAFS and machine-learning interatomic potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07081)
*Vitalijs Dimitrijevs,Pjotrs Žguns,Inga Pudza,Aleksandr Kalinko,Alexei Kuzmin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究结合变温Zn K边扩展X射线吸收精细结构谱（EXAFS）与原子模拟，对典型各向异性金属——六方密堆积（hcp）锌的晶格动力学进行了系统研究。通过反蒙特卡洛方法提取八个配位壳层的均方位移（MSRD），并基于关联爱因斯坦模型揭示面内与面外相互作用的显著各向异性；同时利用CHGNet机器学习原子间势的微调版本，显著改善了对实验EXAFS谱和径向分布函数的描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锌作为一种典型的各向异性金属，其晶格动力学特性（尤其是面内与面外相互作用的差异）需要精细的壳层分辨表征。同时，通用机器学习原子间势在预测热无序方面存在偏差，亟需结合实验数据进行验证与修正。

Method: 采用变温Zn K边EXAFS光谱实验结合原子模拟；利用反蒙特卡洛（reverse Monte Carlo）方法从实验数据中提取八个配位壳层的均方位移（MSRDs），并通过关联爱因斯坦模型（correlated Einstein model）分析MSRD温度依赖关系以获得有效原子间力常数。此外，使用CHGNet通用机器学习原子间势进行分子动力学模拟，并对原模型进行微调以改进热无序描述。

Result: 关联爱因斯坦模型分析揭示了面内与面外相互作用之间显著的各向异性；第一与第二配位壳层MSRD之比与衍射实验得到的各向异性位移参数高度一致。原始的CHGNet模型高估了热无序，而微调后的版本在与实验EXAFS谱和径向分布函数的吻合度上有实质性提升。

Conclusion: EXAFS信息辅助的分析方法能有效验证并精修机器学习原子间势，为各向异性金属晶格动力学的准确描述提供了可靠途径。

Abstract: The lattice dynamics of hexagonal close-packed (hcp) zinc, a prototypical anisotropic metal, is studied using temperature-dependent Zn K-edge extended X-ray absorption fine structure (EXAFS) spectroscopy combined with atomistic simulations. The reverse Monte Carlo method enable the extraction of mean-square relative displacements (MSRDs) for eight coordination shells, providing a shell-resolved description of thermal motion. The MSRD temperature dependence, analysed using the correlated Einstein model, yields effective interatomic force constants and reveals pronounced anisotropy between in-plane and out-of-plane interactions. This anisotropy is further quantified by the ratio of MSRDs for the first and second coordination shells, which closely matches the anisotropic displacement parameters from diffraction experiments. Molecular dynamics simulations using the CHGNet universal machine-learning interatomic potential show that the original model overestimates thermal disorder, while a fine-tuned version substantially improves agreement with experimental EXAFS spectrum and radial distribution function. Overall, EXAFS-informed analysis is effective for validating and refining machine-learning interatomic potentials.

</details>


### [105] [Net and Hidden Spin-Valley Locking Enable Ultrahigh Hole Mobility in Covalent Bulk WN$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07097)
*Rong-Tian Pang,Zhongjuan Han,Jiayi Gong,Jiangang He,Jin-Jian Zhou,Yugui Yao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过从头算（ab initio）电子-声子和输运计算，证明块体WN₂的两种六方相可实现净自旋-能谷锁定与隐藏自旋-能谷锁定，并在室温下表现出超高空穴迁移率；其中中心对称的β-WN₂通过隐藏Zeeman型自旋极化形成的补偿自旋织构，可像净锁定一样有效抑制能谷间散射，确立了隐藏自旋极化作为输运保护机制的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室温下高载流子迁移率是高性能电子学的基础，但块体半导体中高空穴迁移率仍然稀缺；自旋-能谷锁定（spin-valley locking）可抑制能谷间散射并提升迁移率，然而该机制仅限于具有破缺反演对称性的材料，隐藏自旋极化是否能在补偿自旋织构下保护电荷输运尚不清楚。

Method: 采用从头算电子-声子耦合和输运计算方法，对块体WN₂的两种六方相（非中心对称的α相和中心对称的β相）进行系统分析，考察其自旋织构特征及对能谷间散射的抑制效果。

Result: 非中心对称的α-WN₂中，大的能谷自旋劈裂产生净自旋-能谷锁定，几乎完全消除声子介导的能谷间散射；中心对称的β-WN₂中，隐藏Zeeman型自旋极化产生补偿的、按扇区分辨的自旋织构，其在不同能谷间反转，抑制能谷间散射的效果与净锁定相当；同时，刚性的W-N/N-N共价网络使剩余的能谷内散射保持较弱，二者共同贡献了超高室温空穴迁移率。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了隐藏自旋极化作为一种有效的输运保护机制，并将自旋-能谷工程（spin-valley engineering）拓展至中心对称的块体半导体材料。

Abstract: High carrier mobility at room temperature underpins high-performance electronics, yet high hole mobility remains rare in bulk semiconductors. Spin-valley locking can suppress intervalley scattering and enhance mobility, but it is limited to materials with broken inversion symmetry. Hidden spin polarization offers a possible route beyond this constraint, although whether its compensated spin textures could protect charge transport remains unclear. Using ab initio electron-phonon and transport calculations, we show that the two hexagonal phases of bulk WN$_2$ realize net and hidden spin-valley locking and exhibit ultrahigh room-temperature hole mobilities. In non-centrosymmetric $α$-WN$_2$, a large valley spin splitting produces net spin-valley locking that nearly eliminates phonon-mediated intervalley scattering. In centrosymmetric $β$-WN$_2$, hidden Zeeman-type spin polarization yields a compensated, sector-resolved spin texture that reverses between valleys and suppresses intervalley scattering as effectively as the net locking does. The stiff W-N/N-N covalent network further keeps the remaining intravalley scattering weak. Our results establish hidden spin polarization as an effective transport-protection mechanism and extend spin-valley engineering to centrosymmetric bulk semiconductors.

</details>


### [106] [Morphology Engineering of Mixed Ionic Electronic Conductors through Aqueous Phase Separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07130)
*Siqi Wang,Maria Restrepo,John Linkhorst,Matthias Wessling*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出通过pH诱导的溶液相分离技术工程化PEDOT:PSS:PEI薄膜的内部形貌，构建互连孔道网络，在超过100微米厚度的有机电化学晶体管（OECT）沟道中同时实现了30 mS的高跨导和13 ms的快响应时间（门电压仅0.05 V），突破了增益-速度权衡的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OECT的性能由有机混合离子-电子导体中离子可及性与电子传输的相互作用决定；增加沟道厚度虽能提升跨导但延长离子传输时间，形成众所周知的增益-速度权衡（gain-speed trade-off），亟需寻找有效途径来缓解这一固有矛盾。

Method: 通过pH诱导的水相相分离（aqueous phase separation）调控PEDOT:PSS:PEI薄膜的内部形貌，构建互连孔道网络以促进电解质渗透并增加电化学可及体积；同时结合DMSO处理和退火工艺增强PEDOT富集电子相的连续性与有序性。

Result: 多孔OECT沟道在超过100微米厚度下实现30 mS跨导与13 ms响应时间，栅电压低至0.05 V；通过对比电子电导率相近但孔结构不同的薄膜，证明形貌是决定器件性能的主导因素，并支持从表面受限调制向空间分布式混合离子-电子传输的转变。

Conclusion: 本工作确立内部形貌（internal morphology）为与分子结构和器件几何互补的设计参数，提供了可扩展的水基制备策略，为高性能OECT、柔性生物电子学（soft bioelectronics）及神经形态材料的发展建立了通用框架。

Abstract: The performance of organic electrochemical transistors (OECTs) is fundamentally governed by the interplay between ionic accessibility and electronic transport within organic mixed ionic-electronic conductors. Although increasing channel thickness enhances transconductance, it also prolongs ion transport, resulting in the well-known gain-speed trade-off. Here, we demonstrate that engineering the internal morphology of PEDOT:PSS:PEI films through pH-induced aqueous phase separation provides an effective route to mitigate this limitation. The resulting interconnected pore network promotes electrolyte penetration and increases the electrochemically addressable volume, while DMSO treatment and annealing enhance the continuity and ordering of the PEDOT-rich electronic phase. Consequently, porous OECT channels achieve a transconductance of 30 mS and a response time of 13 ms at an ultralow gate voltage of 0.05 V despite channel thicknesses exceeding 100 um. Comparison of films with comparable electronic conductivity but different pore architectures identifies morphology as the dominant factor governing device performance, supporting a transition from predominantly surface-limited modulation toward spatially distributed mixed ionic-electronic transport. Beyond demonstrating a scalable water-based fabrication strategy, this work establishes internal morphology as a design parameter that complements molecular structure and device geometry in organic mixed conductors, providing a general framework for the development of high-performance OECTs, soft bioelectronics, and future neuromorphic materials.

</details>


### [107] [Homojunction-induced thermopower enhancement in polymer films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07266)
*Zhen Xu,Hui Li,Guangzheng Zuo,Xiaojuan Dai,Jincheng Liao,Guofeng Cheng,Jian Song,Wenqing Zhang,Martijn Kemerink,Lidong Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文报道了一种通过在导电聚合物中构建具有不同掺杂浓度的面内分段均匀结（homojunction）结构，在不明显降低电导率（σ）的前提下显著增强塞贝克系数（S）的方法。在p型PDPP-Se分段薄膜中实现了室温下Z T值1.36的纪录，归因于加热时均匀结处产生的额外电压。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 导电聚合物作为新兴热电材料已受到关注超过二十年，但电导率（σ）与塞贝克系数（S）之间的相互制约（trade-off）一直阻碍其在实际器件中的应用。作者旨在寻找一种能够同时提升两者或突破此权衡关系的策略。

Method: 作者构建了由两侧不同掺杂水平组成的p型和n型有机热电腿的面内分段均匀结结构，在施加正向温度梯度（加热重掺杂一侧）时测量其热电性能。同时采用动力学蒙特卡洛模拟（kinetic Monte Carlo simulation）解释增强的塞贝克系数的微观机制。

Result: 对于p型PDPP-Se两段式分段薄膜，获得了异常的S值210 μV K⁻¹和σ值2.5×10⁴ S m⁻¹，功率因数（power factor, PF）达到1100 μW m⁻¹ K⁻²，室温下实现了创纪录的ZT值1.36。正向温度梯度下S异常高于各组分平均值，反向温度梯度下则低于平均值。

Conclusion: 塞贝克系数的增强归因于加热时均匀结处产生的额外电压。该发现为导电聚合物热电输运性质的调控提供了突破性途径。

Abstract: It has been more than twenty years since conductive polymers began to receive attention as an emerging thermoelectric material. However, the trade-off between electrical conductivity (σ) and thermopower (S) has proven to be a major challenge that has obstructed their use in actual devices. Here we report the discovery that the thermopower of the p- and n-type legs of organic thermogenerators can be substantially enhanced, without significant deterioration of σ, by constructing an in-plane segmented structure consisting of a homojunction with different doping levels on either side. In such segmented layers, the S is abnormally higher than the average value of the constituent parts when applying a forward temperature gradient (heating the heavily doped counterpart), while it is lower upon a reverse temperature gradient. Typically, for a two-stage segmented film of p-type PDPP-Se, an abnormally large S of 210 uV K-1 and σ of 2.5*10^4 S m-1 are obtained, resulting in a large power factor (PF) of 1100 uW m-1 K-2 and a record ZT of 1.36 at room temperature. The enhanced thermopower is attributed to an additional voltage developed at the homojunction under heating as explained by kinetic Monte Carlo simulations. This finding provides a breakthrough approach to the modulation of thermoelectric transport properties of conductive polymers.

</details>


### [108] [Fractional Spin Ferroelectric and Sliding Spin Current in Magnetic Sliding Ferroelectrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07305)
*Yilin Han,Lei Li,Chaoxi Cui,Run-Wu Zhang,Zhi-Ming Yu,Yugui Yao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文研究了磁性滑移铁电体（magnetic sliding ferroelectrics）中的分数自旋铁电性（fractional spin ferroelectric, FSFE），发现层间滑移可自然产生对称性保护的自旋纯电流（pure spin current），即"滑移自旋电流"。研究在双层CrI₃和2H-VX₂等实际材料中进行了验证，并预测了可实现全电学产生纯自旋电流的具体材料体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在磁性滑移铁电体中，铁电切换不仅涉及面外电极化反转，还伴随面内分数自旋电极化（fractional in-plane spin electronic polarization）的变化。作者旨在探索这种切换过程中是否能够产生纯自旋电流，从而提出一种全新的全电学产生纯自旋电流的机制。

Method: 作者从理论上分析了FSFE中滑移切换的电荷与自旋转移机制，证明价电子与离子对面内电荷转移的贡献相互抵消，而仅来自价电子的面内自旋转移得以保留，从而形成纯自旋电流。研究在多个材料候选体系中进行验证，包括实验已确认具有磁性SFE性质的双层CrI₃，以及实验已合成的R型堆叠双层2H-VX₂（X=S, Se, Te）。

Result: 对于约1纳秒的典型切换时间，双层CrI₃的自旋电流密度估计达到10⁹(ħ/2e) A/m²，VX₂体系达到10⁸(ħ/2e) A/m²。研究表明，通过施加周期性面外电场，可以在磁性滑移铁电体中产生显著的交流自旋电流。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了一种全电学产生纯自旋电流的新机制，即在磁性滑移铁电体中通过周期电场激发滑移自旋电流，并给出了可供实验验证的具体真实材料预测。

Abstract: We investigate the fractional spin ferroelectric (FSFE) in magnetic sliding ferroelectrics (SFEs), where ferroelectric switching is characterized not only by the reversal of the out-of-plane electric polarization but also by a variation of fractional in-plane spin electronic polarization. We show that interlayer sliding in FSFEs can naturally lead to a symmetry-protected pure spin current, termed the sliding spin current here. The underlying mechanism is that, during switching, the contributions of valence electrons and ions to the in-plane charge transfer cancel each other, whereas the in-plane spin transfer, which stems solely from valence electrons, persists, leading to a pure spin current. We demonstrate our ideas in various material candidates, including $H$-stacked bilayer CrI$_3$, whose few-layer form has been experimentally confirmed to be a magnetic SFE, and $R$-stacked bilayers $2H$-V$X_2$ ($X=$ S, Se, Te), which have been experimentally synthesised. For a typical switching time of about $1$ ns, the estimated spin-current densities for bilayer CrI$_3$ and V$X_2$ reach $10^9 (\hbar/2e)\mathrm{A/m^2}$ and $10^8 (\hbar/2e)\mathrm{A/m^2}$, respectively. This means that by applying a periodic out-of-plane electric field, a significant alternating spin current can be generated in magnetic SFEs. Thus, our findings propose a compelling new mechanism for the all-electrical generation of pure spin current, and predict concrete realistic materials for experimental verification.

</details>


### [109] [Surface Equilibration Mechanism Controls the Stability of a Model Co-deposited Glass Mixture of Organic Semiconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07320)
*Shinian Cheng,Yejung Lee,Junguang Yu,Lian Yu,M. D. Ediger*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究了通过物理气相沉积（physical vapor deposition, PVD）制备的二元有机半导体玻璃（TPD与m-MTDATA质量比50:50）的热力学与动力学稳定性，发现在基板温度Tsub=0.78-0.90Tg范围内可以获得与最稳定单组分有机玻璃相当的超稳定玻璃，且表面平衡机制同样适用于共沉积体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究确定了通过物理气相沉积制备超稳定单组分有机玻璃的条件，但对共沉积混合体系（co-deposited mixtures）的稳定性知之甚少，因此有必要系统研究二元共沉积玻璃的稳定性行为。

Method: 制备了TPD与m-MTDATA两种有机半导体的二元PVD玻璃（质量浓度50:50），在一系列基板温度（Tsub）范围内沉积，并使用差示扫描量热法（differential scanning calorimetry）和光谱椭偏仪（spectroscopic ellipsometry）评估其焓稳定性和动力学稳定性。

Result: 在Tsub=0.78-0.90Tg条件下沉积时，二元有机半导体玻璃表现出与最稳定单组分有机玻璃相当的热力学和动力学稳定性；当在0.94Tg沉积时，m-MTDATA/TPD玻璃的焓值等于该温度下平衡液体的预期值。

Conclusion: 此前针对单组分PVD玻璃提出的表面平衡机制（surface equilibration mechanism）同样适用于共沉积玻璃，这为设计高性能有机电子器件提供了新途径。

Abstract: While previous work has identified the conditions for preparing ultrastable single-component organic glasses by physical vapor deposition (PVD), little is known about the stability of co-deposited mixtures. Here, we prepared binary PVD glasses of organic semiconductors, TPD (N,N-Bis(3-methylphenyl)-N,N-diphenylbenzidine) and m-MTDATA (4,4,4-Tris[phenyl(m-tolyl)amino]triphenylamine), with 50:50 mass concentration over a wide range of substrate temperatures (Tsub). The enthalpy and kinetic stability are evaluated with differential scanning calorimetry and spectroscopic ellipsometry. Binary organic semiconductor glasses with exceptional thermodynamic and kinetic stability comparable to the most stable single-component organic glasses are obtained when deposited at Tsub=0.78-0.90Tg (where Tg is the conventional glass transition temperature). When deposited at 0.94Tg, the enthalpy of m-MTDATA/TPD glass equals that expected for the equilibrium liquid at that temperature. Thus, the surface equilibration mechanism previously advanced for single-component PVD glasses is also applicable for these co-deposited glasses. These results provide an avenue for designing high-performance organic electronic devices.

</details>


### [110] [DynaCrys: Crystal Generation with Dynamic Space-Group Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07401)
*Zhuotao Jin,Xiaoyun Wang,Nicholas Brawand,Roman Zubatyuk,Atul Thakur,Eric Qu,Boris Kozinsky,Justin Smith*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: DynaCrys是一种晶体生成模型（generative model），通过耦合符号扩散过程（coupled symbolic diffusion process）让空间群（space group）与Wyckoff占位和元素共同演化，在晶体结构搜索中实现了最优的综合性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新晶体材料的搜索涉及巨大的组成和结构空间，需要同时建模离散的晶体学对称性、元素组成和连续几何。现有方法难以在同一生成框架内统一处理这些异构信息，尤其在空间群随结构演化时缺乏合理约束。

Method: DynaCrys引入耦合符号扩散过程，使空间群与Wyckoff占位和元素共同演化；空间群转移遵循晶体学群-子群关系（group-subgroup relations）。方法采用共享的预训练对称性码本（symmetry codebook），为合法性约束的随机解码器和对称性约束的晶体几何模型提供统一的Wyckoff词汇表示。

Result: 在两个独立的弛豫与评估引擎（relaxation-and-evaluation engines）的大规模评估中，DynaCrys在对称性感知的稳定、独特且新颖晶体的发现方面取得了最高性能（best-in-class），包括额外要求非平凡弛豫后对称性的情形。该模型还支持快速采样，并持续产生弛豫诱导结构位移（relaxation-induced structural displacements）较低的结构。

Conclusion: DynaCrys通过将空间群演化嵌入晶体生成过程，显著提升了对称性感知的晶体发现能力，同时兼顾了生成速度和结构稳定性，为晶体材料的计算搜索提供了有效的新工具。

Abstract: The search for new crystalline materials spans an enormous compositional and structural space. Generating candidates in this space requires jointly modeling discrete crystallographic symmetry, elemental composition, and continuous geometry. We introduce DynaCrys, a generative model for crystals in which the space group co-evolves with Wyckoff occupations and elements through a coupled symbolic diffusion process. The structured space-group transitions follow crystallographic group-subgroup relations. As the space group changes, a shared, pretrained symmetry codebook provides both the legality-constrained stochastic decoder and the symmetry-constrained crystal-geometry model with a common representation of the corresponding Wyckoff vocabulary. Across large-scale evaluations using two independent relaxation-and-evaluation engines, DynaCrys achieves best-in-class performance in symmetry-aware discovery of stable, unique, and novel crystals, both overall and under the additional requirement of nontrivial post-relaxation symmetry. It also enables fast sampling while generating structures with consistently low relaxation-induced structural displacements.

</details>


### [111] [Permutation invariant neural network prediction of vacancy formation under deformation and varying chemical environment in FCC high entropy alloys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07445)
*Tanvir Sohail,Swarnava Ghosh*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一个原子尺度信息置知、排列不变的机器学习框架（permutation invariant machine learning framework），用于从局部原子环境预测面心立方（FCC）高熵合金（high-entropy alloys, HEAs）中随应变变化的空位形成能（vacancy formation energies），实现了比直接原子模拟快数个数量级的准确预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空位形成能在高熵合金中主导扩散、辐照损伤、相稳定性及动态失效等过程，但其对局部化学环境和机械变形的强依赖性使得大规模原子级计算代价过高，亟需高效的预测方法。

Method: 提出一个原子尺度信息和排列不变（permutation invariant）的机器学习框架，采用以空位为中心的表示（vacancy-centered representation），由客观几何描述符及局部变形梯度的不变量（invariants of the local deformation gradient）构成，从而在统一框架内学习化学无序与有限变形（finite deformation）的耦合效应。

Result: 原子模拟表明体积变形是控制空位形成能平均变化的主导因素，而剪切变形影响相对较小；同时，在相同宏观加载条件下仍存在显著的位点间（site-to-site）变异性，说明局部化学环境主导空位能量的统计分布。该框架在不同变形状态下准确预测空位形成能，且评估速度比直接原子模拟快数个数量级。

Conclusion: 该工作为将应力依赖的缺陷能量学纳入化学复杂合金中扩散、辐照损伤和动态失效的多尺度模型提供了高效途径。

Abstract: Vacancy formation energies govern diffusion, irradiation damage, phase stability, and dynamic failure in high-entropy alloys (HEAs), yet their strong dependence on local chemical environments and mechanical deformation makes atomistic calculations prohibitively expensive for large-scale studies. Here, we develop an atomistically informed permutation invariant machine learning framework for predicting strain dependent vacancy formation energies in FCC HEAs from local atomic environments. The model employs a vacancy-centered representation constructed from objective geometric descriptors together with invariants of the local deformation gradient, enabling the coupled effects of chemical disorder and finite deformation to be learned within a unified framework. Atomistic simulations reveal that volumetric deformation is the dominant factor controlling the average variation in vacancy formation energy, whereas shear deformation has a comparatively minor influence. At the same time, substantial site to site variability persists under identical macroscopic loading, demonstrating that local chemical environments govern the statistical distribution of vacancy energetics beyond species-averaged trends. The proposed framework accurately predicts vacancy formation energies across diverse deformation states while providing orders-of-magnitude faster evaluation than direct atomistic simulations. These results establish an efficient route for incorporating stress-dependent defect energetics into multiscale models of diffusion, irradiation damage, and dynamic failure in chemically complex alloys.

</details>

### [112] [Post-Poisson algebras, extended $\mathcal{O}$-operators and extended Poisson Yang-Baxter equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06736)
*Yuanchang Lin,Dilei Lu*

Main category: math.RA

TL;DR: 本文在泊松代数（Poisson algebras）上引入了扩展 $\mathcal{O}$-算子（extended $\mathcal{O}$-operators）作为普通 $\mathcal{O}$-算子的自然推广，并由此定义扩展泊松杨-巴克斯特方程（extended Poisson Yang-Baxter equations），同时系统地研究了它们与后泊松代数（post-Poisson algebras）、上边界泊松双代数（coboundary Poisson bialgebras）及泊松杨-巴克斯特方程之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 普通 $\mathcal{O}$-算子在泊松代数上已有丰富研究，但其推广形式和相应方程尚不完整。本文旨在通过引入扩展 $\mathcal{O}$-算子与扩展泊松杨-巴克斯特方程，进一步揭示泊松代数结构中算子与方程的深层联系。

Method: 首先定义权重为 $\lambda$ 的扩展 $\mathcal{O}$-算子，证明其可诱导后泊松代数结构，对应操作代数为泊松代数操作欧的“三后继”（trisuccessor）；利用对称化-反对称化分解（symmetrizer-antisymmetrizer decomposition）给出扩展 $\mathcal{O}$-算子的等价刻画；引入扩展泊松杨-巴克斯特方程，并建立其与上边界泊松双代数及扩展 $\mathcal{O}$-算子的联系；通过张量形式定义扩展方程，并在一类二次泊松代数（quadratic Poisson algebras）与半直积泊松代数（semi-direct product Poisson algebras）框架下综合比较各对象间关系。

Result: 研究表明权重 $\lambda$ 的 $\mathcal{O}$-算子导出后泊松代数，其操作欧为泊松代数操作欧的三后继；扩展 $\mathcal{O}$-算子可在模空间上诱导新的泊松代数结构；通过对称化-反对称化分解获得了扩展 $\mathcal{O}$-算子的等价特征刻画；扩展泊松杨-巴克斯特方程作为泊松杨-巴克斯特方程的推广被建立，并阐明了其与扩展 $\mathcal{O}$-算子及上边界泊松双代数之间的关联。

Conclusion: 扩展 $\mathcal{O}$-算子和扩展泊松杨-巴克斯特方程构成了泊松代数理论中普通 $\mathcal{O}$-算子与泊松杨-巴克斯特方程的自然深化与推广，三者之间的关系在二次和半直积泊松代数框架下得到了统一阐明。

Abstract: This paper introduces the extended $\mathcal{O}$-operators on Poisson algebras as a natural generalization of ordinary $\mathcal{O}$-operators, together with the extended Poisson Yang-Baxter equations. We show that $\mathcal{O}$-operators of weight $λ$ on Poisson algebras give rise to post-Poisson algebras, whose operad are the trisuccessor of the operad of Poisson algebras, and that extended $\mathcal{O}$-operators induce new Poisson algebra structures on module spaces.Equivalent characterizations of extended $\mathcal{O}$-operators are obtained via the symmetrizer-antisymmetrizer decomposition. The generalized Poisson Yang-Baxter equations are also introduced, and their connections with coboundary Poisson bialgebras and extended $\mathcal{O}$-operators are established. The tensor form of extended $\mathcal{O}$-operators leads to the notion of the extended Poisson Yang-Baxter equations, which generalizes the notion of the Poisson Yang-Baxter equations. Finally, the relationships among extended $\mathcal{O}$-operators, the extended Poisson Yang-Baxter equations, and the Poisson Yang-Baxter equations are studied in the framework of quadratic Poisson algebras and semi-direct product Poisson algebras.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.quant-gas'></div>

### [113] [Acoustic fluctuations of two-dimensional dipolar supersolids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06800)
*P. Blair Blakie*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文通过Bogoliubov–de Gennes计算，研究了具有三角晶格和蜂窝晶格序的二维偶极超固体（dipolar supersolid）中长波长声学激发的微观结构，开发了一种从微观密度扰动中提取晶格位移的程序，从而识别出横波和两个纵波分支。分析揭示了下纵波模式中应变与缺陷密度涨落的大幅抵消现象，并证明长波长涨落振幅与完全由超固体弹性系数决定的流体动力学理论一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解二维偶极超固体中声学激发的微观物理机制，以及这些激发如何关联晶体（crystalline）和超流体（superfluid）自由度。

Method: 使用Bogoliubov–de Gennes计算来确定三角晶格和蜂窝晶格超固体中三种声学模式所对应的密度、相位、电流和晶格位移涨落；开发了一种从微观密度扰动中直接提取晶格位移的数值程序；将密度涨落分解为晶体应变和相对晶格的粒子输运两部分贡献。

Result: 识别出三种声学模式分别对应一个横波（transverse）分支和两个纵波（longitudinal）分支

Conclusion: 长波长涨落振幅与由弹性系数完全确定的流体动力学理论一致

Abstract: We investigate the microscopic structure of the long-wavelength acoustic excitations of two-dimensional dipolar supersolids with triangular and honeycomb crystal order. Using Bogoliubov--de Gennes calculations, we determine the density, phase, current, and lattice-displacement fluctuations associated with the three acoustic modes. We develop a procedure to extract the lattice displacement directly from the microscopic density perturbation, allowing the modes to be identified as one transverse and two longitudinal branches. The displacement field further separates the density fluctuations into contributions from crystal strain and particle transport relative to the lattice. This reveals that the lower longitudinal mode can have large strain and defect-density fluctuations that substantially cancel in the total density response. We show that the long-wavelength fluctuation amplitudes agree with hydrodynamic theory, which is specified entirely by the elastic coefficients of the supersolid. Our analysis provides a microscopic connection between Bogoliubov excitations and the crystalline and superfluid degrees of freedom of two-dimensional supersolids, and identifies their signatures in density- and current-sensitive probes.

</details>


### [114] [Extracting the pairing gap from van Hove singularities in rf spectra of the Fermi Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06883)
*Chuping Li,Kaichao Zhang,Junru Wu,Yuxuan Wu,Dingli Yuan,Pengyi Chen,Lin Sun,Qijin Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文提出利用三维吸引型费米-哈伯德模型中射频谱的范霍夫奇异性来确定配对能隙，并通过两个奇异性位置的简单代数关系同时提取配对能隙和化学势，无需完整光谱拟合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子模拟的费米-哈伯德系统中，传统的背弯（back-bending）方法在强耦合区域灵敏度下降，需要一种更稳健的光谱诊断手段来提取配对能隙。

Method: 对三维吸引型费米-哈伯德模型射频谱中的范霍夫奇异性进行分类，推导其谱位置与配对能隙Δ和化学势μ之间的简单代数关系，并通过数值模拟验证动量积分和kz积分谱中这些奇异性在寿命展宽和散射展宽下仍然可见。

Result: 四类奇异性被分類，测量两个分辨良好的奇异性即可同时确定Δ和μ。在强耦合区域，这些特征更加显著，而传统背弯方法在该区域灵敏度降低。在半填充时，粒子-空穴对称性固定了μ，只需测量单一奇异性即可完成提取。

Conclusion: 范霍夫奇异性分析被确立为量子模拟三维费米-哈伯德体系中配对的一种实用光谱诊断工具。

Abstract: We show that van Hove singularities in rf spectra of the 3D attractive Fermi Hubbard model provide a robust route to extracting the pairing gap. Four types of singularities are classified, and their spectral positions are shown to depend solely on the pairing gap $Δ$ and chemical potential $μ$ through simple algebraic relations. Measuring two well-resolved singularities therefore determines both parameters without requiring full spectral fitting. Numerical simulations incorporating phenomenological lifetime and scattering broadenings confirm that these features remain visible in both momentum-integrated and $k_z$-integrated spectra, and become more pronounced at stronger coupling where conventional back-bending methods lose sensitivity. At half filling, particle-hole symmetry fixes $μ$, reducing the extraction to a single singularity measurement. These results establish vHS analysis as a practical spectroscopic diagnostic for pairing in quantum-simulated 3D Fermi Hubbard systems.

</details>


### [115] [Observation of far-from-equilibrium scaling in the transient dynamics of 2D quantum magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07211)
*Fabio Bensch,Umberto Borla,Federico Balducci,Philip Osterholz,Shuanghong Tang,Silpa Baburaj Sheela,Anushya Chandran,Roderich Moessner,Jad C. Halimeh,Christian Groß*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文利用可编程里德伯原子阵列（programmable Rydberg-atom arrays）在蜂巢、方格、Kagome和三角晶格上研究横场伊辛模型（transverse-field Ising model）的淬灭动力学，发现磁化振荡的软化与阻尼率极大值标志着相互作用主导与场主导瞬态动力学之间的交叉，且振荡频率和阻尼率在按配位数加权相互作用强度重新标度后塌缩到共同曲线上，揭示了远非平衡二维短程相互作用量子磁体的标度规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远非平衡量子多体动力学的瞬态区间缺乏平衡态中普适性和标度所提供的组织原则，尤其在二维短程相互作用系统中，均场近似预期失效、可控理论描述稀少且涨落强烈，是理解最薄弱的领域。作者旨在探索该区域是否存在可识别的标度行为和组织原则。

Method: 采用可编程里德伯原子阵列实现蜂巢、方格、Kagome和三角四种晶格几何上的横场伊辛模型淬灭动力学实验，从完全磁化态出发观察集体磁化振荡行为；同时对比离散截断Wigner近似（discrete truncated Wigner approximation）与树张量网络（tree-tensor-network）模拟结果。

Result: 观察到主导集体磁化振荡的显著软化伴随阻尼率极大值，标志相互作用主导与场主导瞬态动力学之间的交叉；不同晶格几何下振荡频率与阻尼率经配位数加权相互作用强度标度后塌缩至共同曲线；均场描述可有效再现磁化振荡，但离散截断Wigner近似在强相互作用下无法预测阻尼，而树张量网络模拟能准确重现动力学。

Conclusion: 结果表明短程相互作用二维量子磁体的瞬态动力学存在稳健的标度区间，即使在强量子涨落存在下，主导瞬态动力学仍可由简单的集体描述所支配，这为发现远非平衡量子物质中的组织原则提供了重要见解。

Abstract: The transient regime of far-from-equilibrium quantum many-body dynamics lacks the established organizing principles that universality and scaling provide in equilibrium. It is least understood for two-dimensional short-range interacting systems, where mean-field arguments are not expected to hold, controlled theoretical descriptions are few, and fluctuations are strong. Here we investigate the quench dynamics of the transverse-field Ising model using programmable Rydberg-atom arrays realizing honeycomb, square, kagome, and triangular lattices. Starting from a fully magnetized state, we observe a pronounced softening of the dominant collective magnetization oscillation accompanied by a maximum in the damping rate, signaling a crossover between interaction- and field-dominated transient dynamics. Even though the microscopic lattice geometries are different, both the oscillation frequencies and the damping rates collapse onto common curves after being rescaled by the coordination-number-weighted interaction strength. Our findings show that a mean-field description effectively reproduces the magnetization oscillations. The importance of correlated quantum fluctuations is underlined by the failure of the discrete truncated Wigner approximation to predict the damping for strong interactions, while tree-tensor-network simulations reproduce the dynamics accurately. These results reveal a robust scaling regime governing the transient dynamics of short-range interacting two-dimensional quantum magnets. They reveal that the dominant transient dynamics is governed by a simple collective description despite the presence of strong quantum fluctuations --- an important insight in the quest to uncover organizing principles in far-from-equilibrium quantum matter.

</details>


### [116] [Crossing over universal scaling laws in two-dimensional driven dissipative condensates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07242)
*Q. Fontaine,F. Helluin,M. Escalera,D. Pinto Dias,A. Lemaître,M. Morassi,M. Wouters,A. Minguzzi,L. Canet,S. Ravets,J. Bloch*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文通过在半导体光学微腔中研究二维驱动耗散极化激元凝聚体（driven-dissipative polariton condensates）的时空相干性质，观察到了从Edwards-Wilkinson（EW）到Kardar-Parisi-Zhang（KPZ）普适类（universality class）之间的交叉，并证实其一阶关联函数符合两种普适类的标度函数，临界指数与理论预测一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低维系统中，涨落增强会阻止连续对称性的自发破缺，导致时空关联函数在大尺度下衰减。虽然平衡态二维玻色凝聚体（如Berezinski-Kosterlitz-Thouless普适类）已被广泛研究，但非平衡态玻色凝聚体的普适行为更为多样且仍有许多开放问题，因此亟需对其时空相干性进行系统探索。

Method: 研究在半导体光学微腔中制备二维驱动耗散极化激元凝聚体，通过调节微观参数系统性地改变系统状态，测量其一阶关联函数（first-order correlation），并检验测量结果与EW和KPZ普适类的标度函数是否吻合，同时提取临界指数与理论值进行对比。

Result: 实验观察到了两种标度定律之间的交叉，分别对应EW和KPZ普适类；测量的一阶关联函数在两种普适类的标度函数上均实现了数据塌缩（collapse），获得的临界指数与理论预测值高度吻合。

Conclusion: 研究结果凸显了极化激元凝聚体固有的非平衡本质，并将其确立为可控探索二维KPZ普适类的理想实验平台。

Abstract: In low dimensional systems, fluctuations are enhanced and prevent the spontaneous breaking of continuous symmetries. As a result, spatial and temporal correlation functions decay at large distances and long times. A well established example is given by two-dimensional bosonic condensates at equilibrium, which do not display long-range order of the coherence but algebraic decay belonging to the Berezinski-Kosterlitz-Thouless universality class. In contrast, the universal behaviors of non-equilibrium bosonic condensates are more diverse and many open questions remain. Here, we explore the spatio-temporal coherence properties of two-dimensional driven-dissipative polariton condensates in semiconductor optical microcavities. By tuning microscopic parameters, we observe a cross-over between two scaling laws that we attribute to the Edwards-Wilkinson (EW) and the Kardar-Parisi-Zhang (KPZ) universality classes. We demonstrate the collapse of the measured first-order correlations onto the EW and KPZ universal scaling functions and obtain critical exponents, well matching the values predicted theoretically. Our results highlight the intrinsic non-equilibrium nature of polariton condensates and establish them as a platform of choice for controlled exploration of the two-dimensional KPZ universality class.

</details>


### [117] [Phase-Noise-Induced Heating in Optical Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07442)
*Jean Paul Nohra,Cyprien Daix,Joris Verstraten,Maxime Dixmerias,Tim de Jongh,Bruno Peaudecerf,Fabrice Gerbier,Tarik Yefsah*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文通过实验与理论相结合的方式，解耦光学晶格中强度噪声与相位噪声对加热效应的贡献，发现相位噪声（尤其对轻原子和深晶格）可成为主导加热源，并提出了基于激光相位噪声功率谱密度预测加热速率的理论框架，该框架在锂-6原子三角晶格实验中得到了准确验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在光学晶格（optical lattice）实验中，加热效应是限制量子模拟精度的重要因素，而强度噪声（intensity noise）传统上被视为主要影响因素。然而相位噪声（phase noise）的贡献尚未被充分分离和定量评估，其在不同晶格参数下的相对主导地位缺乏系统的理论与实验研究。

Method: 作者采用实验与理论相结合的方法，通过解耦（disentangle）强度噪声与相位噪声在不同晶格参数（如原子质量和晶格深度）下的各自作用，建立了一个简化的理论框架，利用可实验测量的激光相位噪声功率谱密度（power spectral density of the laser phase noise）来预测由相位噪声引起的加热速率。

Result: 结果表明，相位噪声可成为主导的加热来源，尤其对于轻原子和深光学晶格而言。基于该理论框架的预测能够准确复现锂-6（lithium-6）原子在三角晶格（triangular lattice）中测得的加热速率。

Conclusion: 该理论方法可被推广应用于其他晶格几何构型和其他原子种类，为光学晶格实验中的噪声控制与加热抑制提供了新的理论指导。

Abstract: We experimentally and theoretically study the origin of heating in optical lattices by disentangling the respective roles of intensity and phase noise depending on the lattice parameters. While intensity noise is widely identified as a major limiting factor, we show that phase noise can become the dominant heating source, especially for light atoms and deep optical lattices. We provide a simple theoretical framework to predict the phase-noise-induced heating from the power spectral density of the laser phase noise, which can be measured experimentally. We show that such predictions can accurately reproduce the measured heating rates of lithium-6 atoms in a triangular lattice. Our approach is readily generalized to other lattice geometries and atomic species.

</details>

### [118] [A model and a finite element approximation of the mixed-dimensionality diffusion problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06976)
*Ignacio Romero,David Portillo*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种边值问题（boundary value problem）的公式化描述，用于建模内部嵌入一维扩散纤维的三维扩散固体（diffusive solid）的耦合行为，并给出了该问题的变分表述、适定性证明、有限元离散化（finite element discretization）及收敛性证明。该近似方法最显著的特点是固体与纤维的网格无需保形（conforming），数值实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维扩散固体与一维扩散纤维耦合行为的建模在材料科学中具有重要意义，但目前缺乏系统的数学表述和严格的数值方法保证。

Method: 首先提出一个边值问题来描述三维固体与一维纤维的耦合扩散行为；然后引入变分表述（variational statement），将耦合扩散场视为在耦合约束下的能量极小化问题，即鞍点问题（saddle-point problem），并证明其适定性（well-posedness）；随后引入有限元离散化，证明有限元解向精确解的收敛性；最后通过数值算例验证理论结果。

Result: 证明了所提出的鞍点问题是适定的，有限元离散解的收敛性得到严格证明，且关键特征在于固体的三维网格与纤维的一维网格不需要保形一致。数值实验证实了理论结果的正确性。

Conclusion: 该工作为三维扩散固体中嵌入一维扩散纤维的耦合问题提供了严格的数学框架和可靠的有限元求解方法，非保形网格的特性为实际工程应用提供了更大的网格灵活性。

Abstract: We present the formulation of a boundary value problem that models the coupled behavior of a three-dimensional diffusive solid with one-dimensional diffusive fibers embedded inside it. We introduce a variational statement of the problem that identifies the linked diffusive fields as energy minimizers under a coupling constraint. This saddle-point problem is proved to be well posed. Then, we introduce a finite element discretization of the proposed boundary value problem, and we prove the convergence of the finite element solution to the exact one. The most significant feature of this approximation is that the meshes of the bodies need not be conforming. Numerical examples confirm the theoretical results

</details>

# nlin.CD [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [Learning a quantitative criterion for distinguishing chaos from noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07109)
*Jaesung Choi,Athokpam Langlen Chanu,Jong-Min Park*

Main category: nlin.CD

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于储层计算（reservoir computing）与交叉预测（cross-prediction）框架的纯数据驱动方法，用于区分时间序列中的混沌（chaos）与噪声（noise），通过比较真实与预测的未来变化之间的平方皮尔逊相关系数来实现判别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分混沌与噪声在时间序列分析中本质上极为困难，因为两者都表现出不规则波动且共享许多统计与动力学特征。现有方法存在两大局限：时间相关噪声可能产生虚假的混沌特征；标量时间序列分析往往需要显式选择嵌入参数（embedding parameters）。

Method: 方法基于储层计算框架并结合交叉预测方案：模型被训练为从变量的当前值预测其未来变化，从而将短期可预测性检验与确定性流的平滑性检验相结合。储层计算的递归结构使模型能够从标量时间序列中有效预测高维混沌动力学，无需显式延迟坐标重构；交叉预测框架则强烈抑制由噪声产生的虚假预测相关性。

Result: 将方法应用于多种合成与经验时间序列：混沌系统在真实与预测未来变化间产生强相关，而噪声过程在低相关区域清晰分离。该方法对实际数据中的测量噪声、有限数据长度和预测滞后增加等限制表现出显著稳健性。

Conclusion: 平方皮尔逊相关系数（squared Pearson correlation coefficient）为直接从观测时间序列区分混沌与噪声提供了一个简单且定量的判别准则。

Abstract: Distinguishing chaos from noise using time-series data is fundamentally challenging because both exhibit irregular fluctuations and share many statistical and dynamical characteristics. Existing methods face two key limitations: temporally correlated noise can yield spurious signatures of chaos, and analyses of scalar time series often require explicit choices of embedding parameters. Here, we propose a purely data-driven method for distinguishing chaos and noise based on a reservoir-computing framework with a cross-prediction scheme. In the proposed approach, the model is trained to predict the future change of a variable from its current value, thereby combining a short-term predictability test with a test of the smoothness of deterministic flows. The recurrent structure of reservoir computing enables effective prediction of high-dimensional chaotic dynamics even from scalar time series without explicit delay-coordinate reconstruction, while the cross-prediction framework strongly suppresses spurious predictive correlations arising from noise. We apply the proposed method to diverse synthetic and empirical time series. Chaotic systems consistently yield strong correlations between the true and predicted future changes, whereas noise processes remain clearly separated in a low-correlation regime. The method also exhibits substantial robustness to practical limitations in empirical data, including measurement noise, limited data length, and increasing prediction lag. These results demonstrate that the squared Pearson correlation coefficient provides a simple quantitative criterion for distinguishing chaos from noise directly from observed time-series data.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.dis-nn'></div>

# cond-mat.dis-nn [[Back]](#toc)

### [120] [Many-Body Mobility Edge and Non-Hermitian Skin Effect in an Interacting Quasi-Periodic Spin Chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07083)
*Lavoisier Wah,Ayan Banerjee,Flore K. Kunst*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该论文研究了受准周期纵向场驱动的相互作用非厄米自旋链，发现了一个"D形"多体迁移率边（many-body mobility edge），它同时划定了多体局域化（many-body localization）与多体趋肤效应（many-body skin effect）的边界，并通过多种诊断量绘制了统一相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非厄米多体物理揭示了拓扑、局域化与边界效应之间的丰富相互作用，但它们在相互作用无序系统中的集体行为仍未得到充分探索。作者旨在提供一个统一且可控的框架，以理解非厄米动力学、相互作用与局域化机制相互交织的物理。

Method: 作者构建了一个受准周期纵向场驱动的相互作用非厄米自旋链，运用分形维数（fractal dimension）、复本征值分数（complex eigenvalue fractions）和多体逆参与率（many-body inverse participation ratios）等诊断量来刻画本征态性质，并结合复能级间距统计（complex level-spacing statistics）与密度不平衡、纠缠增长、波包演化等动力学观测量，绘制统一的相图。

Result: 研究发现了"D形"多体迁移率边，可将扩展态与局域化本征态分离，同时标定多体局域化与多体趋肤效应的区域。趋肤效应在多体非厄米本征态中诱导了多重分形标度（multifractal scaling），所有诊断量一致确定了该迁移率边的存在。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了一个清晰的多体迁移率边特征，并强调了它在塑造开放量子多体系统物理中的关键作用，为理解非厄米多体系统的相行为提供了统一的图景。

Abstract: Non-Hermitian many-body physics reveals a rich interplay between topology, localization, and boundary effects, yet their collective behavior in interacting disordered systems remains largely unexplored. In this work, we study an interacting non-Hermitian spin chain subject to a quasi-periodic longitudinal field, providing a unified and controlled setting, where non-Hermitian dynamics, interactions, and localization mechanisms intertwine. Remarkably, we discover a "D-shaped" many-body mobility edge that separates extended and localized eigenstates, while simultaneously delineating regimes of many-body localization and the many-body skin effect (where many-body eigenstates acquire an anomalous drift towards a boundary under open boundaries) emerging from the combined action of interactions, non-Hermiticity, and driving amplitude. We demonstrate that the skin effect induces multifractal scaling in the non-Hermitian eigenstates, providing a clear signature of the many-body skin effect. Employing diagnostics such as the fractal dimension, complex eigenvalue fractions, and many-body inverse participation ratios, we map out a unified phase diagram in which all measures consistently identify the "D-shaped" mobility edge. Finally, we probe this interplay using both complex level-spacing statistics and dynamical observables such as density imbalance, entanglement growth, and wave-packet evolution, culminating in a rich many-body mobility phase diagram that captures both the many-body skin effect and localization transitions. Our results identify a clear, defining signature of the "D-shaped" many-body mobility edge, and underscore its pivotal role in shaping the physics of open quantum many-body systems.

</details>
