### [121] [The structural relaxation time of a polymer glass during deformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06575)
*Pradip K. Bera,Grigori A. Medvedev,James M. Caruthers,Mark D. Ediger*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本研究通过在稳态塑性流动（steady-state plastic flow）范围内对轻度交联的聚甲基丙烯酸甲酯（PMMA）玻璃进行应变率切换（strain rate switching）实验，结合光学探针重定向（optical probe reorientation）方法量化链段运动，发现变形过程中的结构松弛时间（structural relaxation time）约等于链段相关时间（segmental correlation time），且Chen-Schweizer模型可定性预测应力响应与链段相关时间的变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为确定聚合物玻璃（polymer glass）在变形过程中的结构松弛时间，以往的实验测量存在歧义，需要一种明确的定量手段来表征变形状态下的动态行为。

Method: 在稳态塑性流动范围内执行应变率切换实验，使用轻度交联的PMMA玻璃，并同时通过光学探针重定向方法量化玻璃中的链段运动（segmental motion）。

Result: 应变率切换后观察到非单调应力响应（non-monotonic stress response），与先前工作一致；链段运动的相关时间呈单调演化至新的稳态，提供了变形过程中结构松弛时间的无歧义测量，其值近似等于链段相关时间。

Conclusion: Chen-Schweizer模型可定性预测链段相关时间变化与非单调应力响应；实验结果与聚合物变形建模中使用的材料时间（material time）假设合理一致，即聚合物玻璃对大变形响应可由线性响应模型结合随时间变化的链段相关时间描述。

Abstract: In order to determine the structural relaxation time of a polymer glass during deformation, a strain rate switching experiment is performed in the steady-state plastic flow regime. A lightly cross-linked poly (methyl methacrylate) (PMMA) glass was utilized, and simultaneously the segmental motion in the glass was quantified using an optical probe reorientation method. After the strain rate switch, a non-monotonic stress response is observed, consistent with previous work. The correlation time for segmental motion, in contrast, monotonically evolves towards a new steady-state, providing an unambiguous measurement of the structural relaxation time during deformation, which is found to be approximately equal to the segmental correlation time. The Chen-Schweizer model qualitatively predicts the changes in the segmental correlation time and the observed non-monotonic stress response. In addition, our experiments are reasonably consistent with the material time assumption used in polymer deformation modeling; in this approach, the response of a polymer glass to a large deformation is described by combining a linear-response model with a time-dependent segmental correlation time.

</details>


### [122] [Non-uniqueness of restitution coefficients in oblique impacts of discs, even in cases of unique normal restitution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06675)
*Dominik Krengel*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文通过数值研究，分析了耗散摩擦圆盘在不同冲击角（impact angle）θ和摩擦系数（friction coefficient）μ对恢复系数（coefficient of restitution）e的影响，发现切向恢复系数e_T和动能恢复系数e_kin呈非单调非线性行为，在μ相关的θ处存在明显极小值，且所有μ在大θ时收敛，边界由纯滚动和纯滑动情形界定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 颗粒材料中的碰撞恢复性质对体系宏观行为至关重要，但现有理论对摩擦圆盘冲击角度依赖性的认识不够深入，缺乏对不同耗散机制的全面描述。

Method: 对耗散摩擦圆盘在不同冲击角θ和摩擦系数μ下进行数值模拟，系统扫描参数空间，考察法向耗散相互作用和初始角速度ω的影响。

Result: e_T和e_kin随θ呈现非单调非线性变化，在μ依赖的θ处出现清晰极小值；所有μ在大θ时收敛，并受纯滚动与纯滑动情形界定的边界约束；改变法向耗散相互作用会影响大θ处的μ收敛并导致低θ处出现收敛；初始角速度ω会显著改变急陡冲击下e_T的函数行为，轻微影响e_kin的幅度，而摩擦仅起次要作用。

Conclusion: e_T和e_kin的任何取值都具有高度情境依赖性，相同数值可能对应完全不同的碰撞构型，说明仅凭单个恢复系数值无法唯一确定碰撞过程。

Abstract: We investigate the different coefficients of restitution $e$ of a dissipative, frictional disc as a function of the impact angle $θ$ and the friction coefficient $μ$. We observe a non-monotonic, non-linear behaviour of $e_{\mathrm{T}}$ and $e_{\mathrm{kin}}$ with a clear minimum at $μ$-dependent values of $θ$ and a convergence for all $μ$ at large $θ$, bounded by the cases of pure rolling and pure sliding. Changing the dissipative normal interaction affects the $μ$ convergence at large $θ$ and leads to a convergence at low $θ$. The presence of initial angular velocity $ω$ can significantly alter the functional behaviour of $e_{\mathrm{T}}$ at steep impacts and slightly change the magnitude of $e_{\mathrm{kin}}$, with friction playing only a minor role. Overall, our results indicate, that any value of $e_{\mathrm{T},\mathrm{kin}}$ is highly situational and can describe entirely different configurations.

</details>


### [123] [Particle Contacts Generate Fractional Density Relaxation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06797)
*Hu Cang*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文针对可逆布朗系统（reversible Brownian systems）中稠密液体弛豫的起始问题，证明硬粒子接触在构型空间中充当反射边界，唯一决定密度弛豫前导项 t^{3/2} 的振幅，并推导出该振幅的表面公式。该公式包含平衡接触概率、法向迁移率和密度灵敏度，对单分散硬球体系可仅由静态结构因子 S(k)、径向接触值 g(σ^+) 和短时扩散系数 D_0 精确预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密液体弛豫经历了从局域粒子碰撞到协同结构重排的演化过程，但短时接触物理如何在不受后续多体重排修正的情况下，为记忆核（memory kernel）提供精确的起始点，一直是核心未解问题。

Method: 作者通过证明硬粒子接触在构型空间中充当反射边界（reflecting boundaries）来刻画前导 t^{3/2} 密度弛豫振幅；利用扩散采样接触边界层（厚度 O(√t)）与局域密度响应的结合机制，推导出振幅的表面公式；并通过 Gram–Schur 投影层级证明常规集体变量保持前导接触振幅严格不变。

Result: 推导出显示式表面公式，将 t^{3/2} 振幅表示为平衡接触概率、法向迁移率和密度敏感度的函数；对单分散硬球，给出仅依赖静态结构 S(k)、径向接触值 g(σ^+) 和短时扩散 D_0 的无拟合精确预测；并将构造推广至软界面情形。

Conclusion: 该框架将微观碰撞动力学与笼效应（caging）及玻璃态结构弛豫直接联系起来，为散射实验、分子模拟和记忆核重建提供了精确的微观边界条件。

Abstract: Dense-liquid relaxation evolves from local particle collisions to cooperative structural rearrangements. While hard-sphere kinetics determines an early $t^{3/2}$ fractional decay in density correlation functions, collective theories describe the subsequent structural relaxation. A central open question has been how short-time contact physics supplies an exact starting point for the memory kernel governing later times without being modified by subsequent many-body rearrangements. Here we resolve this problem for a broad class of reversible Brownian systems. We prove that hard particle contacts act as reflecting boundaries in configuration space, uniquely dictating the amplitude of the leading $t^{3/2}$ density relaxation. Mechanistically, diffusion samples a contact boundary layer of thickness $O(\sqrt{t})$, which combines with the local density response to produce the fractional signal. We derive an explicit surface formula expressing this amplitude in terms of equilibrium contact probability, normal mobility, and density sensitivity. For monodisperse hard spheres, this yields an exact, fit-free prediction determined entirely by static structure $S(k)$, radial contact value $g(σ^+)$, and short-time diffusion $D_0$. Extending the construction, we determine the corresponding normalization for soft interfaces and prove via a Gram--Schur projection hierarchy that regular collective variables leave the leading contact amplitude strictly invariant. The resulting formulation connects microscopic collision kinetics directly to caging and glass-like structural relaxation, providing an exact microscopic boundary condition for scattering experiments, molecular simulations, and memory-kernel reconstructions.

</details>


### [124] [Active Brownian motion in a single-relaxation viscoelastic fluid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07059)
*Sanatan Halder,Manas Khan*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该论文将原有的"慢扩散谐波阱模拟单弛豫黏弹性流体"模型扩展到活性布朗粒子运动的研究，揭示了自驱动持续时间与流体黏弹性的特征时间尺度之间的相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活性布朗粒子（active Brownian particles, ABPs）在黏弹性（viscoelastic, VE）介质中展现出由自推进、热涨落和流体黏弹性共同决定的丰富动力学现象。物理黏弹性流体的特性难以独立调控，因而需要发展一种可调谐的黏弹性环境来系统研究其主动布朗运动。

Method: 作者将原有模型中"布朗动力学在缓慢扩散的谐波阱内运动"等同于"在单弛豫黏弹性流体中运动"的对应关系推广至活性粒子的情形。通过解析预测与模拟相结合，分析由VE流体的交叉时间（crossover time）和弛豫时间（equilibration time）$τ_k$和$λ$，以及ABP的持续运动时间（persistence time）$τ_{\mathrm{R}}$控制的动力学行为。

Result: 论文研究了两个实际相关的极限区域：由活性运动持续性和黏弹性流体弹性约束主导的动力学。使用光敏活性Pt包覆Janus胶体在动态光陷阱中的实验，结果与模拟定量吻合。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种黏弹性环境，其黏弹性性质可调谐且不受自推进强度影响，从而能够以物理黏弹性流体无法实现的方式系统研究活性布朗粒子在黏弹性介质中的运动。

Abstract: Active Brownian particles (ABPs) in viscoelastic (VE) media exhibit fascinating dynamical phenomena set by self-propulsion, thermal fluctuations, and fluid viscoelasticity. We extend our model, in which the Brownian dynamics within a slowly diffusing harmonic well emulates that in a single-relaxation VE fluid, to study active Brownian motion in such media. Consequently, the resultant dynamics is governed by the interplay of the characteristic timescales of the systems: the crossover and equilibration times of the VE fluid, $τ_k$ and $λ$, respectively, and the persistence time of the ABP, $τ_{\mathrm{R}}$. Following analytical predictions and simulations, we study two practically relevant regimes where the dynamics is dominated by the persistence of active motion and the elastic confinement of the VE fluid, with a phoretically active Pt-coated Janus colloid in a dynamic optical trap, and show quantitative agreement with the simulations. This approach provides a VE environment with tunable VE properties that remain unaffected by the strength of self-propulsion, allowing us to systematically investigate active Brownian motion in VE media in ways that are not otherwise possible with physical VE fluids.

</details>


### [125] [Overaging with stress in polymer glasses? Faster segmental dynamics despite larger yield stress!](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07310)
*Masoud Razavi,Enran Xing,M. D. Ediger*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文通过探针取向重排（probe reorientation）技术直接测量了压力老化（stress aging）及随后应力释放过程中PMMA玻璃的链段动力学（segmental dynamics），发现尽管在应力下老化的样品具有更高的屈服应力（yield stress），但其应力释放后的链段动力学比静态老化（quiescent aging）样品更快，这与"过度老化"（overaging）的传统解释相矛盾，表明屈服应力并非结构弛豫时间的简单函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究表明，中等水平应力下老化会导致比静态老化更高的屈服应力，这一现象被解释为应力加速物理老化（physical aging），即"过度老化"。然而，这一解释缺乏对老化期间及老化后链段动力学的直接测量验证，因此需要通过直接实验手段检验该假设是否成立。

Method: 研究采用探针取向重排技术直接测量PMMA玻璃在应力老化过程中及应力释放后的链段动力学，并与无应力（静态）老化样品的动力学行为进行对比。

Result: 在应力下老化的样品虽然具有更高的屈服应力，但应力释放后其链段动力学比静态老化的样品更快。这一观察结果在所探索的条件范围内与"过度老化"的解释相矛盾。

Conclusion: 结果表明屈服应力并非结构弛豫时间（structural relaxation time）的简单函数，基于这一理解的理论模型需要修正。

Abstract: It is well known that physical aging of polymer glasses increases their yield stress and affects their failure behavior. Studies indicate that application of moderate levels of stress during aging results in higher yield stress compared to aging in the absence of stress (quiescent aging). This has been interpreted to indicate that stress accelerates physical aging, and has been described as overaging. In this study, we age PMMA glasses under stress, and carry out direct measurement of segmental dynamics during and after aging by using a probe reorientation technique. We observe that samples aged under stress, despite having higher yield stress, have faster segmental dynamics after stress release than quiescently aged samples. This contradicts the overaging interpretation, for the range of conditions explored here. Our results indicate that yield stress is not a simple function of structural relaxation time and theoretical models based on this understanding need to be revised.

</details>


### [126] [Segmental Dynamics in the Strain-hardening Regime for Poly(methyl methacrylate) Glasses with and without Melt-stretching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07330)
*Enran Xing,Trevor Bennin,Masoud Razavi,M. D. Ediger*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文利用光漂白（photobleaching）技术测量了轻微交联的聚甲基丙烯酸甲酯（PMMA）玻璃在应变硬化（strain-hardening）区的链段动力学，发现熔体拉伸（melt-stretching）后的PMMA玻璃具有更显著的应变硬化特征和约15%更快的链段动力学，且深应变硬化区中非拉伸样品的链段动力学较屈服后加速最高达40%，与已有模拟结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚合物玻璃在大变形过程中表现出应变硬化特性，有助于稳定玻璃抵抗断裂，但实验上对应变硬化过程中的链段动力学（segmental dynamics）知之甚少，而此类数据对构建该变形区域聚合物玻璃的分子级理论至关重要。

Method: 采用光漂白技术，测量了有/无熔体拉伸的轻微交联PMMA玻璃的链段动力学；样品在本地区域工程应变速率10^-4.6 s^-1至10^-4 s^-1下，分别在Tg-23K和Tg-33K温度变形进入应变硬化区。

Result: 熔体拉伸后的PMMA玻璃表现出更显著的应变硬化特征，其链段动力学比未拉伸样品快约15%；在给定的真实应变速率下，未拉伸PMMA的链段动力学在深应变硬化区较屈服后加速最多达40%（在0.8真实应变处），观测结果与已发表的模拟结果一致。

Conclusion: 该实验首次定量表征了应变硬化区的链段动力学行为，并揭示了熔体拉伸状态对动力学加速的影响，为构建聚合物玻璃大变形区的分子级理论提供了关键实验依据。

Abstract: Strain-hardening is a feature of polymer glasses during large deformation, which helps to stabilize the glasses against breakage. Experimentally, little is known about the segmental dynamics during strain-hardening, and such data is important for building a molecular-level theory of polymer glasses deformed in this regime. Here, using a photobleaching technique, we measured the segmental dynamics of lightly-crosslinked poly(methyl methacrylate) (PMMA) glasses with and without melt-stretching, which were deformed into the strain-hardening regime with local engineering strain rates from 10^-4.6 s^-1 to 10^-4 s^-1 at Tg-23K and Tg-33K. We find that melt-stretched PMMA glasses show a more prominent strain-hardening feature and faster segmental dynamics by a factor of about 15% compared to PMMA without melt-stretching. At a given true strain rate, the segmental dynamics of PMMA without melt-stretching are accelerated in the deep strain-hardening regime from the value just beyond yield, by up to 40% at 0.8 true strain. Our observations are in agreement with previously published simulation results.

</details>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [Cascading Through the Hierarchy: Regularizer-Induced Feature Detection as Phase Transitions in Deep Linear Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06597)
*Björn Ladewig,Ibrahim Talha Ersoy,Karoline Wiesner*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过一个解析可解的玩具模型（toy model），以正则化强度为可调外部参数，建立深度学习相变级联与损失景观几何结构之间的严格联系，并从微观Hessian谱与宏观有效描述两个层面给出了可解析的相变预测与序参量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习科学理论（scientific theory of deep learning）正快速发展，而解析可解的玩具模型是其基石之一。此前研究虽已数值/唯象地发现调节正则化强度可引发相变级联（cascade of phase transitions），但缺乏严格的理论框架来支撑这些数值观察。

Method: 以正则化强度作为类统计物理中的外场（external field）参数，对最小模型进行解析求解；通过可解析的序参量（order parameter）刻画可学习特征（learnable features），并将宏观有效描述与微观Hessian谱（Hessian spectrum）几何相联系。

Result: 揭示了相变级联与可学习特征及底层几何结构之间的精确对应关系，给出了这些相变的解析预测以及对应的可解析序参量；在最小模型层面将宏观有效描述与微观损失景观（loss landscape）的Hessian谱几何关联起来。

Conclusion: 所提出的模型为探索和深化根植于统计物理概念的深度学习科学理论提供了一个平台，架起了宏观相变现象与微观几何结构之间的桥梁。

Abstract: A scientific theory of deep learning, comprising learning dynamics and statistical properties of learned models, is rapidly gaining attention. One of the corner stones of this development are analytically solvable toy models, allowing for the fully tractable analysis of the learning dynamics. Here we analytically investigate such a toy model using the regularization strength as a tunable external parameter - akin to external fields in statistical physics. In previous studies, (i) an onset of learning transition was predicted analytically and (ii) it was phenomenologically/numerically established that tuning the regularization strength can result in a cascade of phase transitions. The number of those transitions was linked to the geometry of the loss landscape determined by the model complexity. Setting up a rigorous framework underpinning the previous numerical observations, our investigation reveals a precise connection between those cascades of phase transitions, learnable features and the underlying geometry. We provide analytic predictions of these phase transitions as well as tractable order parameters related to learned features. At the level of the minimal model, we connect this macroscopic perspective (that can be condensed into an effective description) to the microscopic perspective in terms of the geometry of the loss landscape characterized by the Hessian spectrum. Thus, the presented model provides a platform to explore and sharpen advances made in the scientific theory of deep learning rooted in statistical physics concepts.

</details>


### [128] [Statistical stability of random potentials to thermal and quantum activation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07194)
*Luis Filsinger,Roland Willa*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本研究针对高斯随机势能曲面（potential landscape）中势能极小值的统计性质及其对热激活与量子激活的稳定性进行分析，推导出解析表达式来评估势能的概率分布特征（值、斜率、曲率等），并将激活率的期望值与势能的格林函数（Green's function）相关联，从而提出通过激活行为反演势能信息的光谱学方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多物理、化学和生物系统的动力学可简化为状态变量在复杂势能曲面中的运动。前人在势能曲面已知的情况下可确定性描述运动与响应，但当流形（manifold）未知时，需要利用状态变量的静态与动态响应作为光谱学工具来刻画势能景观，本文旨在发展这种未知势能表征方法。

Method: 受Embon等人工作的启发，研究者针对高斯随机流形（Gaussian random manifolds）推导出解析代数表达式，用于评估势能特征（值、斜率、曲率等）的统计概率；在此基础上，计算热与量子激活（thermal and quantum activation）速率的期望值，并将其与高斯势能的主要特征——格林函数——建立联系。

Result: 本文推导出在高斯随机流形中势能特征统计概率的解析表达式，并据此计算出热激活与量子激活速率的期望值，找到这些激活行为与势能格林函数之间的定量联系。

Conclusion: 激活行为与格林函数之间的关联提供了通过研究对象在势能流形中的激活特征来反推势能格林函数信息的途径，从而可作为未知势能景观的光谱表征工具。

Abstract: In numerous physical, chemical, and biological systems the dynamics can be reduced to the motion of state variables in a complex potential landscape. In case the manifold is known, the motion and response of the embedded object can be described deterministically up to stochastic effects usually associated with a noise. In contrast, if the manifold is unknown, the static and dynamic response of the state variable may be used as a spectroscopic tool to characterize the potential landscape. Inspired by a seminal work of L.\ Embon and co-workers, [Sci.\ Rep.\ \textbf{5}, 7598 (2015)] we investigate the statistical properties of potential minima, in particular, their stability to thermal and quantum activation. For Gaussian random manifolds, we derive an algebraic expression to evaluate the statistical probability of the potential character (value, slope, curvature, ...). With this tool, we compute the expectation value for the rate of thermal and quantum activation and link these findings to the principal characteristics of the Gaussian potential, i.e., its Green's function. This link provides the opportunity to access information on the potential's Green's function by studying the activation behavior of an object in this manifold.

</details>


### [129] [Distribution of the Radius of Gyration for an ISAW](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07195)
*Antony Lesage,Vincent Dahirel,Jean-Marc Victor,Maria Barbi*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文推导了相互作用自回避行走（Interacting Self-Avoiding Walk, ISAW）的回转半径（radius of gyration）有限尺寸概率分布的显式自由能表达式，并通过贝叶斯推断与三维蒙特卡罗模拟结果对比验证，成功构建了卷曲-球状转变（coil-globule transition）的相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在为相互作用自回避行走（ISAW）的回转半径R获得一个以链长N和单体-单体相互作用能ε为变量的有限尺寸概率分布显式表达式。此前缺乏能够同时捕捉卷曲-球状转变和有限尺寸标度修正的解析自由能形式。

Method: 首先推导非相互作用自回避行走的显式自由能表达式，引入以密度ρ的幂表示的新自然尺度变量t = ρ^g。随后利用Lhuillier、Victor及其合作者提出的累积量展开（cumulant expansion）方法将其推广至相互作用情形，从而同时纳入卷曲-球状转变、有限尺寸标度修正、熵及表面能贡献。最终通过贝叶斯推断（Bayesian inference）估计模型参数，并与三维ISAW的蒙特卡罗模拟结果进行对比。

Result: 所得回转半径分布的自由能表达式与广泛的三维蒙特卡罗模拟结果在除Θ点（Θ-point）附近之外的区域表现出极好的一致性；在Θ点附近，有限链长限制了可达的标度区域。验证结果支持了自由能表达式的有效性。

Conclusion: 文章验证了所提出的自由能表达式的正确性，并以此为基础构建了卷曲-球状转变的相图，为该类系统的热力学行为提供了解析描述工具。

Abstract: We aim at calculating an explicit expression for the finite-size probability distribution of the radius of gyration $R$ of an Interacting Self-Avoiding Walk (ISAW), as a function of chain length $N$ and monomer-monomer interaction energy $\varepsilon$. We first derive the explicit free energy expression for a non-interacting Self-Avoiding Walk, introducing a new natural scale variable $t = ρ^g$ expressed as a power of the density $ρ$. Then, thanks to a cumulant expansion approach introduced by Lhuillier, Victor and coworkers, we extend it to the interacting case, capturing both the coil-globule transition and finite-size corrections to scaling, including entropic and surface-energy contributions. The radius of gyration distribution determined by the new free energy expression is then compared, using Bayesian inference to estimate the model parameters, to results from extensive Monte Carlo simulations of three-dimensional ISAWs, showing excellent agreement except very close to the $Θ$-point, where finite chain lengths limit the accessible scaling regime. This validates our resulting free energy expression and allows us, as an application, to construct a phase diagram of the coil-globule transition.

</details>


### [130] [Active movement of foraging sea turtles generates anomalous looping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07448)
*Vijay Kumar,Vladimir V. Palyulin,Perla Roman-Torres,Christophe Eizaguirre,Rainer Klages*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究利用卫星追踪数据，为西非海岸捕食的红海龟（loggerhead sea turtles）构建了随机广义朗之万方程（stochastic generalised Langevin equation），发现其活跃运动呈现大规模环状轨迹，这种环状运动在中等时间尺度上产生类似莱维行走（Lévy walks）的超扩散行为，揭示了一种基于环路的主动异常搜索模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动物运动轨迹（如昆虫、鸟类、海洋捕食者等）常呈现看似随机的路径，现有运动模型（相关随机行走、莱维行走、主动粒子动力学）均未纳入从实验轨迹中提取的长期非马尔可夫记忆（non-Markovian memory），因此缺乏对这类复杂模式的数据驱动理解。

Method: 从红海龟卫星追踪数据出发，构建随机广义朗之万方程，将实验轨迹中的长期非马尔可夫记忆纳入运动建模，并对运动特征（如环路尺度、超扩散性）进行分析，与洋流效应与手性（chirality）进行区分。

Result: 红海龟表现出由大规模环状轨迹构成的主动运动，这些环路不归因于洋流或手性效应；环路将运动限制在特定觅食区域内，并在中间时间尺度上产生类似莱维行走的超扩散现象。

Conclusion: 研究识别出一种基于环路的主动异常搜索（active anomalous search）模式，该模式与多种动物物种中观察到的觅食行为一致，可能启发机器人搜索策略和基于人工智能的元启发式优化算法（metaheuristic optimisation algorithms）的设计。

Abstract: Animals inhabiting diverse environments by moving across different spatial scales, from insects to birds, marine predators, mammals and even humans, often display apparently random movement paths. Over the past decade, novel biologging technologies have recorded these patterns in increasing detail, generating a wealth of experimental data. A central challenge is to understand such complex patterns by constructing data-driven mathematical models. Many animal movements depart from Brownian motion, as described by correlated random walks, Lévy walks, or active particle dynamics. Yet, these movement models do not incorporate long-term non-Markovian memory extracted from experimental trajectories. Here, we construct a stochastic generalised Langevin equation from satellite tracking data for loggerhead sea turtles (Caretta caretta) foraging off the coast of West Africa. We find that these turtles exhibit active movement characterised by large-scale loops that are not explained by ocean currents or chirality. These loops maintain movement within a specific foraging region and, over intermediate timescales, generate superdiffusion similar to Lévy walks. We thus identify a loop-based form of active anomalous search related to foraging patterns observed across a wide range of animal species, which may inspire robotic search strategies and AI-based metaheuristic optimisation algorithms.

</details>


### [131] [Entanglement Mpemba Effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07465)
*Ruicheng Bao,Yue Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出了纠缠Mpemba效应（entanglement Mpemba effect）的概念，即在相同开放系统动力学下，初始纠缠度较低的态可以超越初始纠缠度较高的态，率先达到纠缠目标态。作者从弛豫谱推导出反转的一般判据，并通过精确可解模型和囚禁离子方案验证了该效应可作为加速纠缠制备的可行路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耗散制备（dissipative preparation）虽然能通过工程化环境稳健地将系统驱动至纠缠目标态，但弛豫过程往往耗时较长。作者希望在不改变耗散协议的前提下，通过初始态工程来缩短到达高纠缠态所需的时间。

Method: 作者从弛豫谱（relaxation spectrum）出发推导了纠缠序反转的一般判据，该判据在纠缠非单调演化时仍然适用；利用确定性局域操作与经典通信（deterministic local operations and classical communication, LOCC）可达性偏序的反转提供与度量无关的反转纠缠序认证；并通过精确可解模型和囚禁离子（trapped-ion）方案进行验证。

Result: 研究表明，通过选择合适的初始态可以显著缩短达到高纠缠态所需的时间。确定性LOCC可达性偏序的反转为纠缠序反转提供了度量无关（measure-independent）的认证。此外，作者提出了一个实验上可行的囚禁离子协议，可实现纠缠Mpemba效应。

Conclusion: 纠缠Mpemba效应将初始态工程转化为一种无需修改耗散协议即可加速纠缠制备的路径，为量子信息处理中的快速纠缠生成提供了新的理论工具和实验方案。

Abstract: Generating entanglement rapidly and reliably is essential for quantum information processing, communication, and metrology. Dissipative preparation is attractive because engineered reservoirs robustly drive a system toward an entangled target, yet relaxation can carry a substantial time cost. Here we formulate the entanglement Mpemba effect, whereby an initially less entangled state overtakes a more entangled state under the same open-system dynamics. This effect turns initial-state engineering into a route for faster preparation without altering the dissipative protocol. We derive a general criterion for the reversal from the relaxation spectrum, applicable even when entanglement evolves nonmonotonically. A reversal of deterministic local operations and classical communication (LOCC)-reachability preorder provides a measure-independent certificate of reversed entanglement order. Exactly solvable models show that initial-state selection can substantially shorten the time required to reach high entanglement. We further propose an experimentally relevant trapped-ion protocol that can realize the entanglement Mpemba effect.

</details>


<div id='astro-ph.CO'></div>

### [132] [Cosmic birefringence from a joint analysis of ACT and Planck](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06480)
*Johannes R. Eskilt*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文联合分析了普朗克数据发布4（Planck Data Release 4）和ACT数据发布6（ACT Data Release 6）的极化图，利用两台望远镜的交叉功率谱（cross-power spectra），在同时检验尘埃污染等系统效应后，测得宇宙双折射角β = 0.277° ± 0.057°，统计显著性为4.8σ，为暗物质与暗能量可能是超轻赝标量场（ultra-light pseudoscalar field）的理论提供了观测证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙微波背景（cosmic microwave background, CMB）线性偏振面若发生宇称破缺旋转（即宇宙双折射，cosmic birefringence），将超出ΛCDM框架的预测，并可作为暗物质与暗能量通过Chern-Simons项耦合电磁学的信号。此前Planck和ACT数据独立发现非零双折射角的迹象，但尚无联合分析。

Method: 对普朗克数据发布4与ACT数据发布6的极化图进行联合分析，利用两个望远镜之间的交叉功率谱提取宇宙双折射角度β，并将已报道的仪器先验（instrumental priors）纳入考虑；同时执行鲁棒性检验，以评估尘埃EB模污染对结果的干扰。

Result: 联合分析在仪器先验取面值的情况下得到β = 0.277° ± 0.057°，统计显著性为4.8σ；在缓解尘埃发射污染的鲁棒性检验中，排除β = 0°的置信度为3.5σ。若要得到β = 0°，需要尘埃EB建模与仪器先验同时失效。

Conclusion: 联合分析结果强烈支持非零宇宙双折射的存在，但作者指出，在得出强宇宙学结论之前，必须理解数据中尚未解决的系统效应（unresolved systematics）。

Abstract: Dark matter and dark energy could be ultra-light pseudoscalar fields that couple to electromagnetism through a Chern-Simons term. This would cause a parity-breaking rotation of the plane of linearly polarized light from the cosmic microwave background, known as cosmic birefringence, which is not predicted by $Λ$CDM. Recent hints of a non-zero cosmic birefringence angle have been found in both the Planck Data Release 4 and ACT Data Release 6 datasets independently. This work jointly analyzes the polarized maps of both, leveraging the cross-power spectra between the two telescopes. Taking the reported instrumental priors at face value, the joint analysis yields $β= 0.277^{\circ} \pm 0.057^{\circ}$, which is non-zero with a statistical significance of $4.8σ$. A robustness test mitigating contamination from dust emission excludes $β= 0^{\circ}$ at $3.5σ$, and retrieving $β=0^{\circ}$ would require both the dust $EB$ modeling and instrumental priors to fail simultaneously. However, unresolved systematics in the data must be understood before we can draw strong cosmological conclusions.

</details>


### [133] [Say Hello, Wave Goodbye: Gravitational Waves from Hyperbolic PBH-SMBH Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06709)
*Laura Burn,Nelson Christensen,Richard Easther*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究早期宇宙形成的原初黑洞（primordial black holes）作为暗物质候选体，当其沿双曲轨道掠过超大质量黑洞（supermassive black hole，SMBH）时产生的引力波暴信号的可探测性。结果表明，相对于LISA和拟议中的μAres任务，无论是单个引力波暴还是"爆米花"背景（popcorn background），来自未束缚PBH的信号都不太可能被探测到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗物质（dark matter）集中于星系中心，因此若有大量原初黑洞作为暗物质候选体，它们将聚集在超大质量黑洞附近，为引力波辐射提供有利环境。作者旨在评估这些PBH掠过SMBH时产生的引力波暴能否被现有或拟议的引力波探测器观测到。

Method: 考虑质量范围为10的-15次方至10倍太阳质量的PBH，沿双曲轨道（hyperbolic trajectory）经过SMBH，在近心点（periastron）穿越期间产生引力波暴。以人马座A*（Sagittarius A*）为主要目标，同时分析M31*和M87*的类似信号（频率较低），评估单个暴发和"爆米花"背景分别相对于LISA和拟议的μAres任务的探测可能性，并与合理的星系中心暗物质晕密度进行比较。

Result: 结果表明，虽然人马座A*是这类事件的主要候选位置，但M31*和M87*也会产生类似但频率更低的引力波信号。然而，将计算结果与合理的中共晕密度（central halo density）相比，未束缚（unbound）的PBH不太可能产生可探测到的信号。

Conclusion: 未束缚的PBH掠过SMBH时产生的引力波信号，无论是个体暴发还是叠加的"爆米花"背景，相对于LISA和μAres的灵敏度而言都不太可能被探测到，表明这类通道对于探测PBH暗物质而言前景有限。

Abstract: Primordial black holes (PBHs) formed in the early Universe remain a viable dark matter candidate. Since dark matter is expected to be concentrated toward galactic centres, a significant population of PBHs would reside near a supermassive black hole (SMBH), providing a promising environment for gravitational wave emission. We consider PBHs with masses from $10^{-15}$ to $10$ solar masses on hyperbolic trajectories past an SMBH, that produce bursts of gravitational radiation during periastron passage. Sagittarius A* is an obvious location for these events, but we show that both M31* and M87* would generate similar signals, albeit at lower frequencies. We assess the detectability of both individual bursts and "popcorn" backgrounds, relative to LISA and the proposed $μ$Ares mission. Comparing these results to plausible central halo densities, we find that unbound PBHs are unlikely to yield detectable signals.

</details>


### [134] [Optical Signatures of Sgr A* and M87* with Dark Matter Halos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06924)
*Noraiz Tahir,Muhammad Ali Paracha,Mubasher Jamil*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本研究利用事件视界望远镜（Event Horizon Telescope, EHT）对Sgr A*和M87*黑洞阴影的观测数据，系统地比较了冷暗物质（cold dark matter, CDM）与核标量场暗物质（scalar field dark matter, SFDM）两种暗物质晕模型对引力透镜信号的影响。结果表明所有模型均在1.2σ置信区间内与EHT观测的阴影直径相符，但焦散结构分析揭示了两种天体在拓扑特征上的差异，为未来观测提供了可检验的理论预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EHT对超大质量黑洞（supermassive black hole, SMBH）Sgr A*和M87*阴影的成像为研究黑洞周围的强引力场区域（strong-field regime）打开了新的窗口，提供了探测黑洞周围暗物质（dark matter, DM）分布这一独特问题的实验室。

Method: 作者系统研究了两种不同的暗物质晕模型——冷暗物质（CDM）分布和核标量场暗物质（SFDM）分布——对Sgr A*和M87*引力透镜信号的影响。具体计算了光子球（photon spheres）、阴影（shadows）、弱透镜与强透镜观测量以及焦散结构（caustic structures），并利用χ²统计将得到的阴影直径与EHT观测数据进行比较。

Result: 所有模型与EHT测得的阴影直径符合度均在1.2σ以内，χ²差值很小（Δχ² ≲ 1.2）。焦散分析揭示了不同拓扑区域：Sgr A*同时保留切向和径向临界曲线，而M87*由于质量更大可能仅显示切向临界曲线。

Conclusion: 两种暗物质模型在现有EHT精度下与观测数据均一致，但焦散结构的拓扑差异为未来观测提供了清晰的观测信号，可用于区分不同暗物质晕模型对黑洞引力透镜特征的标记性影响。

Abstract: The event horizon telescope (EHT) has opened a new window onto the strong-field regime by imaging the shadows of the supermassive black holes (SMBHs) Sgr A* and M87*. These observations provide a unique laboratory for probing the dark matter (DM) distribution around black holes. In this work we systematically investigate the imprints of two distinct DM halo models, the cold DM (CDM), and the cored scalar field DM (SFDM) profiles on the gravitational lensing signatures of Sgr A* and M87*. We compute the photon spheres, shadows, weak and strong lensing observables, and caustic structures for both models. We then compared the obtained values of the shadow diameters with the EHT data, using $χ^2$ statistics. We found that all the models are within $1.2σ$ of the measured shadow diameters with the small $χ^2$ differences, $Δχ^2 \lesssim 1.2$. The caustic analysis reveals distinct topological regimes, Sgr A* retains both tangential and radial critical curves, while M87* may show only tangential critical curves due to its larger mass. This topological difference provides a clear observational signature for future observations.

</details>


### [135] [Joint Geometric and Dynamical Constraints on Cosmology from Anisotropies in Galaxy Intrinsic-Alignment Correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06927)
*Teppei Okumura*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文首次联合宇宙学分析，利用BOSS光谱数据与DESI传统影像巡天的星系形状测量，从星系内禀排列（intrinsic alignments, IAs）中同时提取几何与动力学信息，通过对各向异性GI和II相关的自旋相关角结构分解与联合建模，将生长率参数fσ₈、角直径距离D_A和哈勃膨胀率H的分数不确定度分别降低32%、18%和29%，确立了各向异性星系形状作为光谱宇宙学额外信息源的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星系内禀排列此前通常被视为引力透镜信号中的系统误差来源，本文的动机在于挖掘其本身蕴含的宇宙学信息。作者试图证明，星系形状的各向异性相关结构不仅可以提供互补于星系聚集（galaxy clustering）的几何信息，还能通过红移空间与Alcock-Paczynski畸变的联合建模同时约束动力学参数。

Method: 使用BOSS光谱数据与DESI传统影像巡天的星系形状测量交叉匹配，覆盖红移范围0.43≤z≤0.7；测量各向异性星系密度-内禀椭率（GI）与内禀椭率-内禀椭率（II）相关函数，将其自旋相关角结构分解为伴随勒让德多极矩（associated Legendre multipoles）；联合建模红移空间畸变（redshift-space distortions）与Alcock-Paczynski畸变以提取宇宙学参数。

Result: 测量的GI相关呈现预期的重子声学振荡（baryon acoustic oscillation, BAO）结构；其各向异性提供互补于星系聚集的几何信息。在约束fσ₈、D_A和H时，联合IA相比仅用星系聚集可分别将三者分数不确定度降低32%、18%和29%。映射到平坦w₀CDM模型后，IA还收紧了w₀、Ω_m和H₀的约束，但w₀约束对分析中包含的最小尺度敏感。

Conclusion: 各向异性星系形状可作为光谱宇宙学中几何与动力学信息的新增来源，为现有星系聚集分析提供互补且显著的精度提升，但尺度截断对暗能量状态方程参数约束的影响需要谨慎考量。

Abstract: We present the first joint cosmological analysis to extract both geometric and dynamical information from galaxy intrinsic alignments (IAs). Using BOSS spectroscopy cross-matched with galaxy shape measurements from the DESI Legacy Imaging Surveys, we measure anisotropic galaxy density--intrinsic ellipticity (GI) and intrinsic ellipticity (II) correlations over $0.43\leq z\leq0.7$ and decompose their spin-dependent angular structure into associated Legendre multipoles. The measured GI correlation exhibits the expected baryon acoustic oscillation (BAO) structure, while its anisotropy provides geometric information complementary to galaxy clustering. By jointly modeling redshift-space and Alcock-Paczynski distortions, we constrain the growth rate parameter $fσ_8$, the angular-diameter distance $D_A$, and the Hubble expansion rate $H$. Relative to galaxy clustering alone, adding IA reduces their fractional uncertainties by 32\%, 18\%, and 29\%, respectively. By mapping these constraints onto a flat $w_0$CDM model, IA also tightens the constraints on $w_0$, $Ω_m$, and $H_0$; however, the $w_0$ constraint is sensitive to the minimum scale included in the analysis. Our results establish anisotropic galaxy shapes as an additional source of geometric and dynamical information for spectroscopic cosmology.

</details>


### [136] [Tensor-Induced Backreaction in Ultra-Slow-Roll Inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07264)
*Alessandro Ciaiolo,Giovanni Marozzi*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了单场暴胀（single-field inflation）模型中张量诱导的逆反应（tensor-induced backreaction）对原初黑洞（primordial black holes）产生的影响，重点分析了二阶微扰论中的修正如何限制慢滚参数ε的抑制并削弱标量功率谱的增强，还通过有效背景演化的闭合方程组对效应做部分重求和，确认了该效应的持续重要性并显著降低了对峰值功率谱幅度的精细调谐需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在暴胀模型中，瞬态超慢滚（ultra-slow-roll）阶段会将共动曲率功率谱放大，从而增强原初黑洞的产生。然而，以往分析通常忽略了张量涨落对背景演化造成的二阶逆反应效应；论文旨在评估该修正是否以及如何改变功率谱增强和原初黑洞产生的结论。

Method: 论文先在二阶微扰论框架下分析张量诱导逆反应对背景度规的修正，重点考察非吸引子区域中一阶慢滚参数ε的抑制被收缩的效果。随后超越标准微扰处理，将背景参数视为有效量，通过自洽的闭合逆反应方程组确定其演化，从而对效应进行部分重求和。

Result: 结果表明，在标准慢滚演化中，领先阶微扰修正通常可忽略，但在非吸引子区域该修正变得显著，限制了ε的抑制并减少了标量功率谱的增强。部分重求和分析确认在考虑背景有效演化后该效应仍然重要。此外，考虑张量诱导逆反应后，与标量功率谱峰值幅度相关的精细调谐（fine-tuning）被大幅降低。

Conclusion: 张量逆反应对瞬态超慢滚模型中原初黑洞的产生具有不可忽视的修正作用，不仅限制了功率谱的增强程度，还有效放宽了对模型参数的精细调谐要求，为更稳健地评估原初黑洞丰度提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We discuss the impact of tensor-induced backreaction at second order in perturbation theory on primordial black holes production in single-field inflationary models, where the comoving curvature power spectrum is amplified by a transient ultra-slow-roll phase. Although the leading perturbative correction is mostly negligible in standard slow-roll evolution, it becomes important in non-attractor regimes, going in the direction of limiting the suppression of the first slow-roll parameter $\varepsilon$ and reducing the enhancement of the scalar power spectrum. We then go beyond the standard perturbative analysis by considering the background parameters as effective quantities with evolution self consistently determined by a closed system of backreaction equations. This analysis, which leads to a partial resummation of the effect beyond the perturbative treatment, confirms the limitation of the suppression of $\varepsilon$ by consistently showing how the effect remains important even after accounting for the effective evolution of the background in the evaluation of the backreaction itself. Finally, we discuss how, taking into account the tensor-induced backreaction, the fine-tuning associated with the value of the peak amplitude of the scalar power spectrum is strongly reduced.

</details>


### [137] [Redshift Dependence of $H_0$ Dipole in Pantheon+ Supernovae](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07209)
*M. H. Jalali-Kanafi,E. Ó Colgáin,S. Pourojaghi,M. M. Sheikh-Jabbari*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文基于Pantheon+第Ia型超新星（Type Ia supernovae）样本，在低红移区间（z_min < z < 0.2）重建了本地宇宙膨胀率H₀的全天方向图，并利用层析分析（tomographic analysis）考察了偶极子（dipole）结构的统计显著性，发现红移下限较低时存在2–3σ的偶极信号，且随z_min增大信号减弱，表明该偶极子是低红移特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准宇宙学模型假定宇宙在大尺度上是均匀各向同性的（isotropic），然而宇宙膨胀率H₀的本地位向性测量可能存在偶极结构，这涉及宇宙学原理（cosmological principle）的检验以及Hubble张力（Hubble tension）问题的潜在来源。作者旨在利用高质量超新星数据系统检验H₀是否在天空中呈现方向性依赖。

Method: 使用Pantheon+编译的Type Ia超新星数据，在CMB参考系（CMB rest frame）中于z_min < z < 0.2范围重建H₀的天区方向图；选取z_min∈[0.015, 0.045]的10个不同值进行层析分析；并以ΛCDM模型生成的1000次蒙特卡洛模拟（Monte Carlo simulation）作为零假设基准来计算统计显著性。

Result: 最低红移区间测得的偶极振幅为A_dip=1.16±0.28 km/s/Mpc，随z_min增大单调递减至A_dip=0.35±0.52 km/s/Mpc。对z_min≲0.032的区间，偶极信号达到2–3σ显著水平；显著区间的偶极方向指向Shapley超星系团（Shapley supercluster）及CMB偶极方向附近。此外，最大对跖点H₀差值ΔH₀^max即使在偶极模型失效后仍保留各向异性残余信号。

Conclusion: H₀偶极子是一个低红移特征（low-redshift feature），随着探测红移范围升高而衰减。该信号的方向与Shapley超星系团和CMB偶极方向一致，但作者未进一步判断该偶极子是宇宙学本征效应还是系统误差所致，仅依据数据呈现结果。

Abstract: We examine the dipole structure in the local cosmic expansion rate $H_0$ using the Pantheon+ compilation of Type Ia supernovae. We reconstruct directional maps of $H_0$ across the sky within the low-redshift regime, $z_{\rm min} < z < 0.2$, evaluated in the CMB rest frame. To perform a tomographic analysis, we choose 10 different values for $z_{\rm min}\in [0.015 , 0.045]$ range. We find a dipole amplitude $A_{\rm dip}=1.16\pm0.28\ {\rm km/s/Mpc}$ for the lowest bin, monotonically decreasing to $A_{\rm dip} =0.35\pm0.52\ {\rm km/s/Mpc}$, as we increase $z_{\rm min}$. Since $A_{\rm dip}$ is positive-definite, by construction $A_{\rm dip} > 0$ even in an isotropic universe, thus to assess the statistical significance, we compare to $A_{\rm dip}$ generated from 1000 Monte Carlo simulations based on the $Λ$CDM model and the same redshift ranges. Our results reveal a $2-3σ$ dipole pattern (depending on how the significance level is computed) for $z_{\rm min}\lesssim 0.032$. For redshift thresholds where the signal remains statistically significant ($>2σ$), the inferred dipole direction points close to Shapley supercluster and CMB dipole directions. As $z_{\rm min}$ increases, the dipole amplitude diminishes in line with expectations of a higher redshift isotropic Universe, but the difference in maximal antipodal $H_0$ determinations, $Δ\text{H}_0^{\rm max}$, retain a signal of an anisotropy that disappears in the dipole ansatz. The decreasing statistical significance of all estimators with increasing $z_{\rm min}$ suggests that any $H_0$ dipole is a low-redshift feature.

</details>


### [138] [Conformal Killing Gravity: New Constraints from DESI DR2 BAO datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07313)
*Himanshu Chaudhary,Salvatore Capozziello,Carlo Alberto Mantica,Luca Guido Molinari,Dhruba Jyoti Gogoi,Ghulam Mustafa*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究一种被称为“共形Killing引力”（Conformal Killing Gravity, CKG）的几何方法，其中暗能量成分由Robertson–Walker时空的共形Killing对称性自然产生。CKG模型仅通过一个额外参数扩展标准ΛCDM宇宙学，并在几何贡献缺失时恢复ΛCDM极限。利用Planck PR4、ACT DR6、DESI DR2及多个Ia型超新星数据集进行约束后，发现该模型支持类quintessence的暗能量演化，且预测未来临界红移在-0.8≲z_c≲-0.7范围，但无法完全解决H₀张力问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统暗能量描述通常依赖经验参数化，缺乏深层物理动机。本文旨在探索一种由时空几何对称性自然导出暗能量行为的替代框架，以提供更简洁且物理动机明确的暗能量描述，同时检验其能否缓解标准宇宙学模型中的观测张力（尤其是H₀张力）。

Method: 在CKG框架下，无散度的共形Killing张量（conformal Killing tensor）充当有效完美流体，其密度、压强和状态方程完全由几何唯一确定。作者以单一额外参数扩展ΛCDM，并利用多类观测数据（Planck PR4 CMB温度、偏振和透镜，ACT DR6 CMB透镜，DESI DR2重子声学振荡，以及Pantheon+、DES-Dovekie、Union3超新星样本）进行统计约束，随后重建暗能量状态方程并分析其演化行为。

Result: 分析表明CKG模型偏好类quintessence的暗能量演化，无phantom穿越证据，重建的状态方程在早期宇宙快速趋近宇宙学常数。该模型预测未来临界红移处于-0.8≲z_c≲-0.7，即宇宙膨胀在z=-1的奇异极限之前达到转折点。由于几何贡献仅改变重组后的膨胀历史，声视界（sound horizon）基本不变，因此无法为H₀张力提供完整解决方案。

Conclusion: CKG提供了一个简单、物理动机明确且被观测数据支持的框架，用于描述晚期宇宙加速膨胀。该模型成功将暗能量动力学嵌入几何结构而不依赖经验参数化，但其在解决H₀张力方面的局限也表明，仅修改后重组时期的膨胀历史不足以彻底调和当前宇宙学中的关键矛盾。

Abstract: We investigate a geometric approach referred to as the Conformal Killing Gravity (CKG), in which the dark energy sector emerges naturally from the conformal Killing symmetry of the Robertson--Walker space-time. Within this framework, the divergence-free conformal Killing tensor behaves as an effective perfect fluid, giving rise to a dynamical dark-energy component whose density, pressure, and equation of state are uniquely determined by the underlying geometry, without introducing any empirical dark-energy parametrization. The resulting CKG model extends the standard $Λ$CDM cosmology through a single additional parameter while recovering the $Λ$CDM limit in the absence of the geometric contribution. We constrain the model using Planck PR4 (NPIPE) CMB temperature, polarization, and lensing observations, ACT DR6 CMB lensing, DESI DR2 baryon acoustic oscillation measurements, and the Pantheon$+$, DES-Dovekie, and Union3 Type Ia supernova compilations. The analysis shows that the CKG favors a quintessence-like dark-energy evolution with no evidence for phantom crossing, while the reconstructed equation of state rapidly approaches the cosmological constant at earlier cosmic times. Furthermore, the model predicts a future critical redshift in the range $-0.8 \lesssim z_c \lesssim -0.7$, indicating that the present cosmic expansion eventually reaches a turning point before the formal singular limit at $z=-1$. Since the geometric contribution modifies only the post-recombination expansion history, the sound horizon remains essentially unchanged and the model does not provide a complete solution to the $H_0$ tension. Our results demonstrate that the CKG provides a simple, physically motivated, and observationally favored framework for describing the late-time accelerated expansion of the Universe.

</details>


### [139] [Sensitivity of Next-Generation CMB Surveys to Neutrinos and Other Light Relics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07453)
*Cynthia Trendafilova,Srinivasan Raghunathan,Benjamin Wallisch,Joel Meyers,Kevork N. Abazajian,Edoardo Altamura,Carlo Baccigalupi,Kimberly K. Boddy,Thejs Brinckmann,Yuji Chinone,Gabriele Coppi,Francis-Yan Cyr-Racine,Jacques Delabrouille,Katherine Freese,Helena García Escudero,Martina Gerbino,Shamik Ghosh,Vera Gluscevic,Daniel Green,Daniel Grin,Kevin M. Huffenberger,Mudit Jain,Lloyd Knox,Anto I. Lonappan,Marilena Loverde,Philip Lubin,Gabriele Montefalcone,Valentine Novosad,Marco Raveri,Christian L. Reichardt,Sayan Saha,Murali M. Saravanan,Emmanuel Schaan,Sara M. Simon,Julien Tang,Scott Watson*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文基于公开的DRAFT（Dark Radiation Anisotropy Flowdown Team）工具，对CMB-S4若干勘测构型在观测有效相对论粒子数N_eff方面的预期灵敏度进行了Fisher矩阵预报。概念设计在七年观测期内可达σ(N_eff)<0.03，修订构型在更长时间尺度上达到相同精度，并辅以宇宙方差极限分析及对早期宇宙物理的广泛讨论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中微子及其他轻 relic（light relics）在宇宙微波背景（CMB）各向异性中留下特征印记，使其成为探测早期宇宙粒子含量与热历史的灵敏探针。在百分之一水平上测量N_eff是CMB-S4等实验长期追求的科学目标，可检验粒子物理标准模型内外的大量物理学假设。

Method: 采用Fisher矩阵预报（Fisher-matrix forecasts）评估多个CMB-S4勘测构型对N_eff的预期灵敏度，并引入宇宙方差极限（cosmic-variance-limited）勘测作为改进空间的下限基准。预报使用公开发布的DRAFT工具完成，该工具提供从模拟前景图与成分分离（component separation）到去透镜（delensing）直至任意宇宙学参数投影灵敏度的端到端流程。

Result: 概念设计勘测构型在七年观测期实现σ(N_eff)<0.03；修订构型在更长观测时间尺度上达到相同精度。宇宙方差极限分析量化了通过额外仪器、观测与理论投入可获得的改进空间。DRAFT工具可直接应用于其他CMB勘测设计的灵敏度预报。

Conclusion: 精确测量N_eff对辐射扇区（radiation sector）、太初核合成（big bang nucleosynthesis）、轻热 relic（light thermal relics）及其他早期宇宙物理学具有广泛意义。DRAFT工具构成一个可复用的端到端预报管线，可用于多种CMB勘测设计评估。

Abstract: Neutrinos and other light relics leave characteristic imprints in the cosmic microwave background anisotropies, making their observation a sensitive probe of the particle content and thermal history of the early universe. The energy density in these relativistic species is parameterized by their effective number $N_\mathrm{eff}$. Measuring this parameter at the percent level, which is a long-standing science goal of CMB-S4 and other experiments, would test a wide range of well-motivated physics within and beyond the Standard Model of particle physics. In this paper, we present Fisher-matrix forecasts of the projected sensitivity to $N_\mathrm{eff}$ of several CMB-S4 survey configurations considered during its extensive design phase. The conceptual design reaches $σ(N_\mathrm{eff}) < 0.03$ over its seven-year observing period, while the revised configuration achieves the same precision over a longer timescale. We complement these results with a cosmic-variance-limited survey over the same multipole range to quantify the room for improvement accessible with additional instrumental, observational, and theoretical efforts. Finally, we discuss the broad implications of precise $N_\mathrm{eff}$ measurements for the radiation sector, big bang nucleosynthesis, light thermal relics, and other early-universe physics. The forecasts presented in this work are performed with the publicly released DRAFT (Dark Radiation Anisotropy Flowdown Team) tool. It provides an end-to-end pipeline from simulated foreground maps and component separation to delensing and projected sensitivities for any cosmological parameter, and it can be directly applied to other cosmic microwave background survey designs.

</details>

### [140] [Emergence of Bogoliubov Fermi Surfaces in hybrid Al/InAs heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06553)
*S. Feyrer,V. Dimic,I. Lobato,A. Kirchner,P. Drexler,L. Rupp,D. Bougeard,T. Lindemann,S. Gronin,G. Gardner,M. J. Manfra,G. F. R. Ruiz,C. A. Balseiro,L. Arrachea,M. Aprili,N. Paradiso,C. Strunk,L. Tosi*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文通过集总元件谐振器（lumped-element resonator）在混合超导体/半导体异质结中研究了邻近化二维电子气的微波电动力学（microwave electrodynamics），测量了超流刚度（superfluid stiffness）随面内磁场的非单调且强各向异性演化，结果表明该行为与Bogoliubov费米面（Bogoliubov Fermi surfaces）的涌现一致，并建立了微波刚度测量作为探测各向异性无隙超导（gapless superconductivity）的灵敏手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合超导体/半导体异质结中的邻近化二维电子气在磁场下的超导响应行为尚未被充分理解，特别是当轨道对破坏（orbital pair breaking）不能单独解释实验观察时，需要新的探测手段来揭示潜在的物理机制。

Method: 使用电感线（inductor wires）相对面内磁场（in-plane magnetic field）定向排列的集总元件谐振器，通过动感电感（kinetic inductance）直接测量超流刚度；结合微观计算超流刚度张量（stiffness tensor），分析塞曼（Zeeman）与轨道Fulde-Ferrell效应对各向异性的联合贡献。

Result: 实验观察到谐振频率随磁场增强呈非单调且强各向异性演化，该行为无法仅由轨道对破坏解释；微观计算表明，这种各向异性源于塞曼效应与轨道Fulde-Ferrell效应的相互作用，并与Bogoliubov费米面的涌现一致，后者根据磁场方向选择性抑制超流响应。

Conclusion: 微波刚度测量被确立为探测混合系统中各向异性无隙超导的灵敏探针，为理解磁场诱导的非传统超导态提供了实验与理论依据。

Abstract: We investigate the microwave electrodynamics of a proximitized two-dimensional electron gas in hybrid superconductor/semiconductor heterostructures. Using lumped-element resonators with inductor wires oriented relative to an in-plane magnetic field, we directly probe the superfluid stiffness via the kinetic inductance. As the field increases, the resonance frequency exhibits a non-monotonic and strongly anisotropic evolution that cannot be explained by orbital pair breaking alone. We show that this behavior is consistent with the emergence of Bogoliubov Fermi surfaces, which selectively suppress the supercurrent response depending on the direction of the magnetic field. Microscopic calculations of the stiffness tensor capture the observed anisotropy driven by the interplay of Zeeman and orbital Fulde-Ferrell effects. Our results establish microwave stiffness measurements as a sensitive probe of anisotropic gapless superconductivity in hybrid systems.

</details>


### [141] [Determination of the properties of a superconducting single crystal FeSe using an EPR spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06602)
*S. I. Bondarenko,A. A. Prokhorov,N. N. Galtsov,V. P. Timofeev,V. P. Koverya,A. V. Krevsun*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文利用电子顺磁共振（EPR）谱仪系统研究了FeSe单晶在超导态（3.5–8K）和正常态（8–25K）的行为，通过非共振信号确定了超导转变温度Tc、转变宽度和第一临界磁场Hc1，并观察到与Fe2+离子顺磁性对应的共振EPR信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FeSe单晶的超导性质（如临界温度Tc与临界磁场Hc1）和其正常态磁性质仍需更全面的实验表征。采用EPR谱仪这一非常规手段，旨在同时获得超导态与正常态的关键磁学参数。

Method: 使用EPR谱仪在最高6000 Oe的磁场下测量FeSe单晶，实验温度覆盖超导态（3.5–8K）与正常态（8–25K），分析零场及弱磁场（最高30 Oe）下的非共振谱仪信号以及高场下的共振信号。

Result: 在零场和弱磁场（最高30 Oe）下通过非共振信号可确定FeSe单晶的Tc、超导转变宽度及第一临界磁场Hc1；在1400 Oe和3400 Oe处观察到共振EPR信号，对应Fe2+的双重电离铁离子的顺磁性。超导态温度范围（3.5–8K）内，非共振EPR信号在最高6000 Oe的磁场下呈非线性函数，对应超导混合态；而在8–25K的正常态区间内，该信号与磁场无关。

Conclusion: EPR谱仪可作为一种有效的实验手段来表征FeSe单晶的超导参数（Tc、转变宽度、Hc1）及其磁态演化，超导态与正常态的非共振信号行为呈现显著差异，反映了混合态特征。

Abstract: Using an EPR spectrometer, the properties of single-crystal FeSe were studied in a magnetic field up to 6000 Oe at temperatures from 3.5 K to 8 K in the superconducting state and at temperatures above 8K and up to 25K in the normal state. It was shown that by measuring non-resonant spectrometer signals in zero and weak magnetic fields up to 30 Oe, it is possible to determine critical temperature Tc, the superconducting transition width, and the value of the first critical magnetic field Hc1 in FeSe single-crystal. Resonant EPR signals are observed in fields at 1400 Oe and 3400 Oe, which corresponds to the paramagnetism of doubly ionized iron atoms (Fe2+) in the FeSe crystal. Non-resonant EPR signals in a magnetic field up to 6000 Oe at temperatures from 3.5K to 8K are a nonlinear function of the field, and correspond to the superconducting mixed state of the FeSe, but at temperatures above 8K (up to 25K) they are field independent.

</details>


### [142] [Origin of the superconductor-insulator transition in disordered two-dimensional films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07101)
*Alexander Weitzel,Lea Pfaffinger,Animesh Panda,Arpan Das,Ilaria Maccari,Simon Reinhardt,Sven Linzen,Evgenii Il'ichev,Nicola Paradiso,Ferdinand Evers,Christoph Strunk*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文通过实验测量二维超导薄膜在宽范围无序强度下的方块电阻$R_\Box(T)$和超流刚度$J_s(T)$，证明即使在超导-绝缘转变（SIT）附近，有限温度相变仍保持Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless（BKT）类型；但随着无序接近临界值，零温度超流相位刚度迅速消失而平均场转变温度保持有限，表明量子相位涨落驱动零温度转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理论预测超导-绝缘转变（superconductor-to-insulator transition, SIT）源于安德森局域化（Anderson localization）与超导电性之间的竞争，但关于有限温度转变在整个无序交叉过程中是否保持BKT特征尚不确定，这构成了本文的核心研究动机。

Method: 本研究在宽范围无序强度$W$下同时测量二维超导薄膜的方块电阻$R_\Box(T)$与超流刚度（superfluid stiffness）$J_s(T)$两条物理量的温度依赖关系，以此追踪无序变化对超导转变性质的影响。

Result: 实验发现在SIT附近，有限温度超导到电阻态的转变仍属于BKT类型；然而当无序接近临界值时，零温度超流相位刚度$J_s(0)$迅速消失，而平均场转变温度$T_{c0}$仍保持有限。

Conclusion: 研究结论表明量子相位涨落（quantum phase fluctuations）是驱动二维薄膜零温度超导-绝缘转变的机制，这一结果在Haviland、Liu和Goldman首次实验发现SIT三十年之后，厘清了其起源。

Abstract: Theory predicts the superconductor-to-insulator transition (SIT) to emerge from the competition between Anderson localization, which tends to localize single-particle wavefunctions, and superconductivity, which establishes long-range correlations in the superconducting order parameter. In two-dimensional (2D) superconducting films, the transition temperature $T_\text{c}$ at which resistance vanishes, $R_\Box(T_\text{BKT}){=}0$, is set by the Berezinskii-Kosterlitz-Thouless (BKT) mechanism and satisfies $T_\text{BKT}< T_{c0}$, where $T_{c0}$ is the mean-field transition temperature. In weakly disordered samples $T_\text{BKT}\lesssim T_{c0}$, whereas increasing disorder drives $T_\text{BKT}\ll T_{c0}$ near the SIT. Whether the finite-temperature transition retains its BKT character throughout this crossover remains an open question. Here, we investigate the evolution of both sheet resistance $R_\Box(T)$ and superfluid stiffness $J_s(T)$ over a wide range of disorder strength $W$. We establish that even near the SIT, the finite-temperature transition from the superconducting to the resistive state remains of BKT type. However, as disorder approaches the critical value, the zero temperature superfluid phase stiffness, $J_s(0)$, is found to vanish rapidly while $T_{c0}$ remains finite, which we attribute to quantum phase fluctuations as the drive for the zero-temperature transition. Three decades after its experimental discovery by Haviland, Liu, and Goldman, our measurements clarify the origin of the SIT in 2D films.

</details>


### [143] [Emergent magnetic pseudogap from phase fluctuations and hierarchy of scales in two-dimensional superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07238)
*Xu-Cheng Wang,Yang Qi*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文从纯相位涨落（phase fluctuations）出发，在不引入任何竞争序（competing order）的前提下，识别出二维超导体正常态中涌现的磁赝隙（magnetic pseudogap），并通过扰动理论计算NMR弛豫率1/T₁T，建立了涵盖电荷与磁赝隙、Hebel-Slichter峰等完整演化行为的统一图像，最终由ξ(T)、ξ_BCS和ℓ三个尺度间的竞争来定量描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究认为预成对（preformed pairs）和相位涨落对二维超导体正常态的电荷赝隙（charge pseudogap）起关键作用。作者希望将此思想推广到磁通道，在不依赖任何竞争序的条件下，从纯相位涨落中识别出磁赝隙（magnetic pseudogap），从而为理解正常态中1/T₁T的抑制提供统一的微观机制。

Method: 在扰动理论的框架下计算NMR弛豫率1/T₁T，分别评估气泡贡献（bubble contribution）和领头阶顶点修正（leading-order vertex correction）。通过分析BKT关联长度ξ(T)、BCS相干长度ξ_BCS以及费米液体散射长度ℓ之间的竞争来刻画不同温度尺度。

Result: 磁赝隙表现为正常态中1/T₁T的平滑抑制，其特征温度尺度T_mPG不同于超导隙Δ_SC和转变温度T_c。电荷与磁赝隙的起始尺度均由ξ(T)与ξ_BCS的竞争主导。顶点修正对d波配对不相关，但在s波系统中变得显著，在略高于T_c的低温下驱动1/T₁T的相干增强；该增强源于s波超导隙边缘发散相干峰，与Hebel-Slichter峰同源，并受费米液体型散射正则化，由ξ(T)与ℓ的竞争决定相干尺度T_coh。

Conclusion: 1/T₁T的完整演化可以在统一图像中被定量理解，即由ξ(T)、ξ_BCS和ℓ三个尺度层次间的相互作用来描述，将磁赝隙、相干增强等现象纳入同一框架。

Abstract: Preformed pairs and phase fluctuations are believed to play a vital role in predicting the charge pseudogap in the normal state of two-dimensional superconductors. In this work, we extend this idea and further identify the emergent magnetic pseudogap from pure phase fluctuations without invoking any competing order. We examine the NMR relaxation rate $1/T_1T$ by evaluating the bubble contribution and leading-order vertex correction within perturbation theory. It is found that the magnetic pseudogap, manifesting as a smooth suppression of $1/T_1T$ in the normal state, is characterized by a temperature scale $T_\text{mPG}$ distinct from the superconducting gap $Δ_\text{SC}$ and transition temperature $T_c$. The onset scales of both charge and magnetic pseudogap are dominated by the competition of BKT correlation length $ξ(T)$ and BCS coherence length $ξ_\text{BCS}$. Moreover, the vertex correction is shown to be irrelevant for $d$-wave pairing, while it becomes prominent in $s$-wave systems and drives a coherent enhancement of $1/T_1T$ at lower temperatures just above $T_c$. We attribute this normal-state enhancement of $1/T_1T$ to the diverging coherence peak at the $s$-wave superconducting gap edge, which shares the same spirit as the celebrated Hebel-Slichter peak in the BCS theory. Analogous to the coherent Hebel-Slichter peak, regularization by Fermi-liquid-like scatterings is important and is characterized by a scattering length $\ell$. The normal-state coherent enhancement of $1/T_1T$ is hence described by the competition of $ξ(T)$ and $\ell$, through which the coherence scale $T_\text{coh}$ is determined. As a result, the complete evolution of $1/T_1T$ is understood quantitatively in a unified picture as the interplay among hierarchy of scales $ξ(T)$, $ξ_\text{BCS}$ and $\ell$.

</details>


### [144] [Onset of the transitional flux-avalanche regime in bulk NbTi controlled by the thermal boundary conductance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07324)
*Irina Abaloszewa,Victor V. Chabanenko,Aleksander Abaloszew*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本研究通过在体块 NbTi 圆盘中引入银填充界面层来增强界面耦合，首次在体块超导体中实现了热磁雪崩阈值场 H_th(T) 的非单调温度依赖性，并在约 6.1–6.4 K 处观察到正斜率区间和最大值，该最大值位置由 NbTi 固有属性决定而与银含量无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有理论预测在过渡区间（界面热导 h 接近临界值 h_c）内 H_th(T) 呈现非单调行为，但在体块超导体中尚未得到实验验证。研究者旨在通过调控界面热耦合来接近该过渡区域，并寻找热磁雪崩（thermomagnetic avalanche）从电磁主导（薄膜中）到热限制（体块中）两态之间转变的直接实验证据。

Method: 在体块 NbTi 圆盘中，通过在纯净十九烷基线界面之上添加银填充界面层来提升界面热耦合系数 h。实验对比了纯净界面与两种不同银填充含量界面的 H_th(T) 依赖关系，并同步观测雪崩形态随温度的变化。

Result: 纯净界面给出单调递减的 H_th(T)；而银填充界面首次在体块超导体中产生非单调温度依赖，在约 6.1–6.4 K 处出现正斜率区间（dH_th/dT > 0）并以最大值 T* 结束。两种银填充成分均复现该效应；在最高银含量的第三种成分中，低温递减完全消失，H_th(T) 平坦直至相同 T*。正斜率区间的起始温度与雪崩形态从窄通道指状向宽前缘的转变一致，而 T* 的位置依赖于 NbTi 固有属性，与银含量无关。

Conclusion: dH_th/dT 符号的反转是过渡区间开始的直接实验标志，此时不稳定过程中的热移除变得动态相关，验证了先前关于界面热导逼近临界值时出现非单调 H_th(T) 的理论预测。

Abstract: Thermomagnetic avalanches in type-II superconductors occur in two qualitatively different regimes, electromagnetically controlled in thin films and thermally limited in bulk samples, distinguished by the sign of the temperature derivative of the threshold field $H_\text{th}(T)$. The two regimes are separated by a critical thermal boundary conductance $h_c$, and a non-monotonic $H_\text{th}(T)$ has been predicted in the transitional region where the interface conductance $h$ approaches $h_c$. We approach this region in a bulk NbTi disk by raising the interface coupling above the pure-nonadecane baseline with a silver-filled interface layer. Whereas the pure interface gives a monotonically decreasing $H_\text{th}(T)$, the silver-filled interface produces a non-monotonic dependence not previously realized in a bulk superconductor: a temperature interval of positive slope, $dH_\text{th}/dT > 0$, terminating in a maximum at $T^* \approx 6.1$--$6.4$~K. The effect is reproduced for two independent silver-filled compositions; in a third, with the highest loading, the low-temperature decrease is absent altogether and $H_\text{th}(T)$ is flat up to the same $T^*$, the evolution expected for stronger coupling. The position of the maximum is set by the intrinsic properties of NbTi, independent of the silver content. The onset of the positive-slope interval coincides in temperature with a change of the avalanche morphology from narrow channeled fingers to broad fronts. The reversal of the sign of $dH_\text{th}/dT$ is a direct experimental signature of the onset of the transitional regime, in which heat removal during the instability becomes dynamically relevant.

</details>

# physics.optics [[Back]](#toc)

### [145] [The Quantitative and dynamical analysis of anisotropic dispersive optical solitons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06484)
*Irfan Mahmood,Memoona Iqbal*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有混合空间与时间演化的广义非线性流体方程的可积性，通过若干可积方法获得广泛的孤立波解，并结合计算模拟、分岔分析与稳定性分析，建立了分析材料非线性能量传播的可靠框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义非线性流体方程被视作描述致密材料（如光纤、金属离子流体）非线性动力学行为的可积工具，并广泛用于解释扰动荷电等离子体中的电磁传播。作者旨在深入探讨该模型的可积方面，并系统分析其孤立波解的理论与数值特性。

Method: 采用若干可积方法（integrable approaches）以获得广泛范围的孤立波解，并辅以计算模拟（computational simulations）以呈现关于能量流动的精确图像。此外，对正则坐标（canonical coordinates）进行分岔分析（bifurcation analysis）以考察参数变化对系统非线性行为的影响。

Result: 通过分岔分析揭示了参数变化对系统非线性行为的调节作用；通过平衡点附近的稳定性分析（stability analysis）评估了光孤子（optical solitons）特征的稳健性。结合数值模拟结果，为材料对能量传播的非线性响应提供了定量认识。

Conclusion: 该研究为通过变动非线性方程模型中的物理参数来分析材料的非线性能量传播响应，建立了可靠且准确的框架。

Abstract: In this article, we explore the integrable aspects of generalized non-linear fluid equation possessing mixed spatial and time evolutions. This non-linear model has been acknowledged as integrable tool for the non-linear dynamical character- ization of dense material such as in optical fibre, metallic ionic fluid. This model widely has been applied in physical interpretations of electromagnetic propaga- tion through perturbed charged plasma. Here, we use few integrable approaches and attain a broad range of solitary solutions involve deep analytical analysis. These results are furnished with computational simulations, which enrich under- standing about the accurate pictorial flow of energy. We also present a bifurcation analysis for canonical coordinates to examine the impact of parameter variations on non-linear behavior of the systems under consideration. Subsequently, we per- form stability analysis to evaluate the robustness of optical solitons features in the vicinity of equilibrium points. These results establish the reliable and accurate framework for analyzing non linear response of materials for energy propagation by varying physical parameters associated with this non-linear equation model.

</details>


### [146] [Geometric Control of Visible Emitter Creation in Hexagonal Boron Nitride by Oblique Ion Irradiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06693)
*Sagar Chowdhury,Bhaveshkumar Kamaliya,Ramachandra Bangari,Caleb Whittier,Joseph Spielbauer,Nabil D. Bassim,Thomas G. Folland,Ravitej Uppu*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文确立了离子入射角（ion-incidence angle）作为调控六方氮化硼（hexagonal boron nitride, hBN）中可见发光缺陷的几何控制参数，通过改变Xe+束的入射角度和剂量，在厚膜中实现最大发射剂量窗口近两个数量级的移动，并借助离子轨迹模拟和AFM表征揭示了其物理机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离子辐照可在宽带隙范德华材料中产生光学活性缺陷，但已有方法主要通过改变离子种类、能量或注量来调控缺陷形成，而未系统考虑入射几何角度的影响。本文旨在将离子入射角确立为一个新的几何控制维度，用于工程化工程化调控hBN中的可见光发射体。

Method: 对不同厚度的hBN薄片施加不同角度和注量的等离子聚焦重离子Xe+束辐照，定量表征辐照后的光致发光（photoluminescence）谱；结合离子轨迹模拟（ion-trajectory simulations）分析碰撞级联的横向再分配和斜射溅射效应；利用原子力显微镜（AFM）识别不同的加工区域；并对辐照样品进行退火处理及光谱分辨的寿命测量。

Result: 厚hBN薄片中，斜入射可将最大发射对应的注量窗口相较于正入射移动近两个数量级；薄片则呈现与角度无关的发射最优值。离子轨迹模拟将此厚度依赖性归因于碰撞级联的横向再分配和增强的斜射溅射。AFM识别出划定了缺陷创建有效窗口的不同加工区域。退火会淬灭发射并将光谱权重移至绿黄波段，同时保持角度依赖的激活趋势。寿命测量显示正、斜入射均呈现类似的双指数动力学。

Conclusion: 离子入射几何可作为一种材料层面的调控旋钮，用于编程范德华光子材料中光学缺陷的激活和空间缺陷分布，而非仅产生几何特定的发射体种类。

Abstract: Ion irradiation creates optically active defects in wide-bandgap van der Waals materials, yet most approaches tune defect formation by varying the ion species, energy, or fluence while leaving the incidence geometry fixed. The ion-incidence angle is established here as a geometric control parameter for engineering visible emitters in hexagonal boron nitride (hBN). The angle and ion fluence of a plasma-focused heavy-ion (Xe+) beam are varied across hBN flakes of different thickness, and the resulting photoluminescence is quantified. In thick flakes, oblique irradiation shifts the fluence for maximum emission by nearly two orders of magnitude relative to normal incidence, whereas thin flakes exhibit an angle-independent optimum. Ion-trajectory simulations attribute this thickness dependence to lateral redistribution of the collision cascade and enhanced oblique sputtering. Atomic force microscopy identifies distinct processing regimes that delineate the useful defect-creation window. Post-irradiation annealing quenches the emission and shifts the spectral weight toward the green-yellow band while preserving the angle-dependent activation trends. Spectrally resolved lifetime measurements show comparable biexponential dynamics for normal and oblique incidence, consistent with emission from related defect families rather than a geometry-specific emitter species. These results establish ion-incidence geometry as a materials-level knob for programming optical defect activation and spatial defect distributions in van der Waals photonic materials.

</details>
